   238→def _shallow_steps(plan: dict) -> list[tuple[str, int, int]]:
   239→    """Return (cluster_name, bare_count, total_count) for clusters with steps lacking detail."""
   240→    results: list[tuple[str, int, int]] = []
   241→    for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
   242→        if cluster.get("auto") or not cluster.get("issue_ids"):
   243→            continue
   244→        steps = cluster.get("action_steps") or []
   245→        if not steps:
   246→            continue
   247→        bare = sum(
   248→            1 for s in steps
   249→            if isinstance(s, dict) and not s.get("detail") and not s.get("issue_refs")
   250→        )
   251→        if bare > 0:
   252→            results.append((name, bare, len(steps)))
   253→    return results
   254→
   255→
   256→def _steps_with_bad_paths(plan: dict, repo_root: Path) -> list[tuple[str, int, list[str]]]:
   257→    """Return steps referencing file paths that don't exist on disk."""
   258→    results: list[tuple[str, int, list[str]]] = []
   259→    for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
   260→        if cluster.get("auto") or not cluster.get("issue_ids"):
   261→            continue
   262→        for i, step in enumerate(cluster.get("action_steps") or []):
   263→            if not isinstance(step, dict):
   264→                continue
   265→            detail = step.get("detail", "")
   266→            if not detail:
   267→                continue
   268→            bad: list[str] = []
   269→            for path_str in _PATH_RE.findall(detail):
   270→                p = repo_root / path_str
   271→                if p.exists():
   272→                    continue
   273→                alt_ext = _EXT_SWAPS.get(p.suffix)
   274→                if alt_ext and p.with_suffix(alt_ext).exists():
   275→                    continue
   276→                bad.append(path_str)
   277→            if bad:
   278→                results.append((name, i + 1, bad))
   279→    return results
   280→
   281→
   282→def _steps_without_effort(plan: dict) -> list[tuple[str, int, int]]:
   283→    """Return (cluster_name, missing_count, total) for steps without effort tags."""
   284→    results: list[tuple[str, int, int]] = []
   285→    for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
   286→        if cluster.get("auto") or not cluster.get("issue_ids"):
   287→            continue
   288→        steps = cluster.get("action_steps") or []
   289→        if not steps:
   290→            continue
   291→        missing = sum(
   292→            1 for s in steps
   293→            if isinstance(s, dict) and s.get("effort") not in _VALID_EFFORTS
   294→        )
   295→        if missing:
   296→            results.append((name, missing, len(steps)))
   297→    return results
   298→
   299→
   300→def _cluster_file_overlaps(plan: dict) -> list[tuple[str, str, list[str]]]:
   301→    """Return pairs of clusters with overlapping file references in step details."""
   302→    cluster_files: dict[str, set[str]] = {}
   303→    for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
   304→        if cluster.get("auto") or not cluster.get("issue_ids"):
   305→            continue
   306→        paths: set[str] = set()
   307→        for step in cluster.get("action_steps") or []:
   308→            if isinstance(step, dict) and step.get("detail"):
   309→                paths.update(_PATH_RE.findall(step["detail"]))
   310→        if paths:
   311→            cluster_files[name] = paths
   312→
   313→    overlaps: list[tuple[str, str, list[str]]] = []
   314→    names = sorted(cluster_files.keys())
   315→    for i, a in enumerate(names):
   316→        for b in names[i + 1:]:
   317→            shared = cluster_files[a] & cluster_files[b]
   318→            if shared:
   319→                overlaps.append((a, b, sorted(shared)))
   320→    return overlaps
   321→
   322→
   323→def _clusters_with_directory_scatter(
   324→    plan: dict, *, threshold: int = 5,
   325→) -> list[tuple[str, int, list[str]]]:
   326→    """Return clusters whose issues span too many unrelated directories.
   327→
   328→    A cluster with issues in 5+ distinct top-level directories is likely
   329→    grouped by theme/dimension rather than by file proximity.
   330→    Returns (cluster_name, dir_count, sample_dirs).
   331→    """
   332→    results: list[tuple[str, int, list[str]]] = []
   333→    for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
   334→        if cluster.get("auto") or not cluster.get("issue_ids"):
   335→            continue
   336→        # Collect file paths from step details
   337→        dirs: set[str] = set()
   338→        for step in cluster.get("action_steps") or []:
   339→            if isinstance(step, dict) and step.get("detail"):
   340→                for path_str in _PATH_RE.findall(step["detail"]):
   341→                    # Use first 2 path components as the "area"
   342→                    # e.g., src/domains/media-lightbox -> src/domains/media-lightbox
   343→                    parts = path_str.split("/")
   344→                    if len(parts) >= 3:
   345→                        dirs.add("/".join(parts[:3]))
   346→                    elif len(parts) >= 2:
   347→                        dirs.add("/".join(parts[:2]))
   348→        if len(dirs) >= threshold:
   349→            results.append((name, len(dirs), sorted(dirs)[:6]))
   350→    return results
   351→
   352→
   353→def _clusters_with_high_step_ratio(
   354→    plan: dict, *, max_ratio: float = 1.0,
   355→) -> list[tuple[str, int, int, float]]:
   356→    """Return clusters where step count >= issue count (1:1 mapping).
   357→
   358→    A well-organized cluster should consolidate related issues into fewer
   359→    steps (because multiple issues touching the same file become one step).
   360→    Returns (cluster_name, steps, issues, ratio).
   361→    """
   362→    results: list[tuple[str, int, int, float]] = []
   363→    for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
   364→        if cluster.get("auto") or not cluster.get("issue_ids"):
   365→            continue
   366→        steps = len(cluster.get("action_steps") or [])
   367→        issues = len(cluster.get("issue_ids") or [])
   368→        if issues >= 3 and steps > 0:  # only flag non-trivial clusters
   369→            ratio = steps / issues
   370→            if ratio > max_ratio:
   371→                results.append((name, steps, issues, ratio))
   372→    return results
   373→
   374→
   375→def _steps_missing_issue_refs(plan: dict) -> list[tuple[str, int, int]]:
   376→    """Return (cluster_name, missing_count, total) for steps without issue_refs."""
   377→    results: list[tuple[str, int, int]] = []
   378→    for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
   379→        if cluster.get("auto") or not cluster.get("issue_ids"):
   380→            continue
   381→        steps = cluster.get("action_steps") or []
   382→        if not steps:
   383→            continue
   384→        missing = sum(
   385→            1 for s in steps
   386→            if isinstance(s, dict) and not s.get("issue_refs")
   387→        )
   388→        if missing:
   389→            results.append((name, missing, len(steps)))
   390→    return results
   391→
   392→
   393→def _steps_with_vague_detail(plan: dict, repo_root: Path) -> list[tuple[str, int, str]]:
   394→    """Return steps with detail too vague to be executor-ready.
   395→
   396→    A step is "vague" if its detail is under 80 chars AND contains no file path.
   397→    Executor-ready means: someone with zero context can read the step and know
   398→    exactly which file to open and what to change.
   399→    """
   400→    results: list[tuple[str, int, str]] = []
   401→    for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
   402→        if cluster.get("auto") or not cluster.get("issue_ids"):
   403→            continue
   404→        for i, step in enumerate(cluster.get("action_steps") or []):
   405→            if not isinstance(step, dict):
   406→                continue
   407→            detail = step.get("detail", "")
   408→            if not detail:
   409→                continue
   410→            has_path = bool(_PATH_RE.search(detail))
   411→            if len(detail) < 80 and not has_path:
   412→                results.append((name, i + 1, step.get("title", "(no title)")))
   413→    return results
   414→
   415→
   416→def _steps_referencing_skipped_issues(plan: dict) -> list[tuple[str, int, list[str]]]:
   417→    """Return steps whose issue_refs include wontfixed/skipped issues."""
   418→    wontfixed = set()
   419→    for fid, issue in plan.get("issues", {}).items():
   420→        if isinstance(issue, dict) and issue.get("status") in ("wontfix", "skipped"):
   421→            wontfixed.add(fid)
   422→    # Also check the wontfix list directly
   423→    for fid in plan.get("wontfix", {}):
   424→        wontfixed.add(fid)
   425→
   426→    if not wontfixed:
   427→        return []
   428→
   429→    results: list[tuple[str, int, list[str]]] = []
   430→    for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
   431→        if cluster.get("auto") or not cluster.get("issue_ids"):
   432→            continue
   433→        for i, step in enumerate(cluster.get("action_steps") or []):
   434→            if not isinstance(step, dict):
   435→                continue
   436→            refs = step.get("issue_refs") or []
   437→            stale = [r for r in refs if r in wontfixed]
   438→            if stale:
   439→                results.append((name, i + 1, stale))
   440→    return results
   441→
   442→
   443→def _enrich_report_or_error(report: str | None) -> str | None:
   444→    if not report:
   445→        print(colorize("  --report is required for --stage enrich.", "red"))
   446→        print(colorize("  Summarize the enrichment work you did:", "dim"))
   447→        print(colorize("  - Which clusters did you add detail/refs to?", "dim"))
   448→        print(colorize("  - Are steps specific enough for an executor with zero context?", "dim"))
   449→        print(colorize("  - Did you link issue_refs so steps auto-complete on resolve?", "dim"))
   450→        return None
   451→    if len(report) < 100:
   452→        print(colorize(f"  Report too short: {len(report)} chars (minimum 100).", "red"))
   453→        print(colorize("  Explain what enrichment you did and why steps are executor-ready.", "dim"))
   454→        return None
   455→    return report

   458→def _require_enrich_stage_for_complete(
   459→    *,
   460→    plan: dict,
   461→    meta: dict,
   462→    stages: dict,
   463→) -> bool:
   464→    if "enrich" in stages:
   465→        return True
   466→    if "organize" not in stages:
   467→        # Fall through to existing organize requirement
   468→        return _require_organize_stage_for_complete(plan=plan, meta=meta, stages=stages)
   469→
   470→    shallow = _shallow_steps(plan)
   471→    if shallow:
   472→        print(colorize("  Cannot complete: enrich stage not done.", "red"))
   473→        print(colorize(f"  {len(shallow)} cluster(s) have steps without detail or issue_refs:", "yellow"))
   474→        for name, bare, total in shallow[:5]:
   475→            print(colorize(f"    {name}: {bare}/{total} steps need enrichment", "yellow"))
   476→        print(colorize(
   477→            '  Fix: desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail "sub-details"',
   478→            "dim",
   479→        ))
   480→        print(colorize('  Then: desloppify plan triage --stage enrich --report "..."', "dim"))
   481→    else:
   482→        print(colorize("  Cannot complete: enrich stage not recorded.", "red"))
   483→        print(colorize("  Steps look enriched. Record the stage:", "dim"))
   484→        print(colorize('    desloppify plan triage --stage enrich --report "..."', "dim"))
   485→    return False
   486→
   487→
   488→def _auto_confirm_enrich_for_complete(
   489→    *,
   490→    plan: dict,
   491→    stages: dict,
   492→    attestation: str | None,
   493→) -> bool:
   494→    if stages.get("enrich", {}).get("confirmed_at"):
   495→        return True
   496→    if "enrich" not in stages:
   497→        return False
   498→    if not attestation or len(attestation.strip()) < _MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN:
   499→        print(colorize("  Cannot complete: enrich stage not confirmed.", "red"))
   500→        print(colorize("  Run: desloppify plan triage --confirm enrich", "dim"))
   501→        print(colorize("  Or pass --attestation to auto-confirm enrich inline.", "dim"))
   502→        return False
   503→
   504→    # Re-validate shallow steps at auto-confirm time
   505→    shallow = _shallow_steps(plan)
   506→    if shallow:
   507→        total_bare = sum(n for _, n, _ in shallow)
   508→        print(colorize(f"  Cannot auto-confirm enrich: {total_bare} step(s) still lack detail or issue_refs.", "red"))
   509→        for name, bare, total in shallow[:5]:
   510→            print(colorize(f"    {name}: {bare}/{total} steps", "yellow"))
   511→        print(colorize('  Fix: desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail "sub-details"', "dim"))
   512→        return False
   513→
   514→    cluster_names = [
   515→        name for name in plan.get("clusters", {}) if not plan["clusters"][name].get("auto")
   516→    ]
   517→    validation_err = _validate_attestation(
   518→        attestation.strip(),
   519→        "enrich",
   520→        cluster_names=cluster_names,
   521→    )
   522→    if validation_err:
   523→        print(colorize(f"  {validation_err}", "red"))
   524→        return False
   525→    stages["enrich"]["confirmed_at"] = utc_now()
   526→    stages["enrich"]["confirmed_text"] = attestation.strip()
   527→    save_plan(plan)
   528→    print(colorize("  ✓ Enrich auto-confirmed via --attestation.", "green"))
   529→    return True

   658→def _completion_clusters_valid(plan: dict, state: dict | None = None) -> bool:
   659→    manual_clusters = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   660→    if not manual_clusters:
   661→        any_clusters = [
   662→            name
   663→            for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
   664→            if cluster.get("issue_ids")
   665→        ]
   666→        if not any_clusters:
   667→            print(colorize("  Cannot complete: no clusters with issues exist.", "red"))
   668→            print(colorize('  Create clusters: desloppify plan cluster create <name> --description "..."', "dim"))
   669→            return False
   670→
   671→    gaps = _unenriched_clusters(plan)
   672→    if gaps:
   673→        print(colorize(f"  Cannot complete: {len(gaps)} cluster(s) still need enrichment.", "red"))
   674→        for name, missing in gaps:
   675→            print(colorize(f"    {name}: missing {', '.join(missing)}", "yellow"))
   676→        print(
   677→            colorize(
   678→                "  Small clusters (<5 issues) need at least 1 action step per issue.",
   679→                "dim",
   680→            )
   681→        )
   682→        print(
   683→            colorize(
   684→                '  Fix: desloppify plan cluster update <name> --description "..." --steps "step1" "step2"',
   685→                "dim",
   686→            )
   687→        )
   688→        return False
   689→
   690→    # Check for unclustered review issues
   691→    unclustered = _unclustered_review_issues(plan, state)
   692→    if unclustered:
   693→        print(colorize(f"  Cannot complete: {len(unclustered)} review issue(s) have no action plan.", "red"))
   694→        for fid in unclustered[:5]:
   695→            short = fid.rsplit("::", 2)[-2] if "::" in fid else fid
   696→            print(colorize(f"    {short}", "yellow"))
   697→        if len(unclustered) > 5:
   698→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(unclustered) - 5} more", "yellow"))
   699→        print(colorize("  Add to a cluster or wontfix each unclustered issue.", "dim"))
   700→        return False
   701→
   702→    return True

   816→    print(colorize("  Current plan:", "bold"))

   818→    if confirmed:

   829→def _note_cites_new_issues_or_error(note: str, si) -> bool:
   830→    new_ids = si.new_since_last
   831→    if not new_ids:
   832→        return True
   833→    valid_ids = set(si.open_issues.keys())
   834→    cited = extract_issue_citations(note, valid_ids)
   835→    new_cited = cited & new_ids
   836→    if new_cited:
   837→        return True
   838→    print(colorize("  Note must cite at least 1 new/changed issue.", "red"))
   839→    print(colorize(f"  {len(new_ids)} new issue(s) since last triage:", "dim"))
   840→    for fid in sorted(new_ids)[:5]:
   841→        print(colorize(f"    {fid}", "dim"))
   842→    if len(new_ids) > 5:
   843→        print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(new_ids) - 5} more", "dim"))
   844→    return False

    33→class ItemOverride(TypedDict, total=False):

   136→class EpicTriageMeta(TypedDict, total=False):
   137→    """Metadata persisted for the multi-stage triage flow."""
   138→
   139→    triaged_ids: list[str]
   140→    dismissed_ids: list[str]
   141→    issue_snapshot_hash: str
   142→    strategy_summary: str
   143→    trigger: str
   144→    version: int
   145→    last_run: str
   146→    last_completed_at: str
   147→    triage_stages: dict[str, TriageStagePayload]
   148→    stage_snapshot_hash: str
   149→    stage_refresh_required: bool
   150→    last_triage: LastTriageSnapshot
   151→
   152→
   153→class PlanModel(TypedDict, total=False):
   154→    version: Required[int]
   155→    created: Required[str]
   156→    updated: Required[str]
   157→    queue_order: list[str]
   158→    deferred: list[str]  # kept empty for migration compat
   159→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry]
   160→    active_cluster: str | None
   161→    overrides: dict[str, ItemOverride]
   162→    clusters: dict[str, Cluster]
   163→    superseded: dict[str, SupersededEntry]
   164→    promoted_ids: list[str]  # IDs user explicitly positioned via move_items()
   165→    plan_start_scores: PlanStartScores
   166→    execution_log: list[ExecutionLogEntry]
   167→    epic_triage_meta: EpicTriageMeta
   168→    commit_log: list[CommitRecord]
   169→    uncommitted_issues: list[str]
   170→    commit_tracking_branch: str | None
   171→    completed_clusters: NotRequired[list[dict[str, Any]]]  # legacy snapshot key
   172→
   173→
   174→def empty_plan() -> PlanModel:
   175→    """Return a new empty plan payload."""
   176→    now = utc_now()
   177→    return {
   178→        "version": PLAN_VERSION,
   179→        "created": now,
   180→        "updated": now,
   181→        "queue_order": [],
   182→        "deferred": [],
   183→        "skipped": {},
   184→        "active_cluster": None,
   185→        "overrides": {},
   186→        "clusters": {},
   187→        "superseded": {},
   188→        "promoted_ids": [],
   189→        "plan_start_scores": {},
   190→        "execution_log": [],
   191→        "epic_triage_meta": {},
   192→        "commit_log": [],
   193→        "uncommitted_issues": [],
   194→        "commit_tracking_branch": None,
   195→    }
   196→
   197→
   198→def ensure_plan_defaults(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   199→    """Normalize a loaded plan to ensure all keys exist.
   200→
   201→    Runtime contract is v8-only. Legacy payloads are upgraded in-place once.
   202→    """
   203→    defaults = empty_plan()
   204→    for key, value in defaults.items():
   205→        plan.setdefault(key, value)
   206→    _upgrade_plan_to_v8(plan)
   207→
   208→
   209→def triage_clusters(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Cluster]:
   210→    """Return clusters whose name starts with ``EPIC_PREFIX``."""
   211→    return {
   212→        name: cluster
   213→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
   214→        if name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX)
   215→    }
   216→
   217→
   218→def validate_plan(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   219→    """Raise ValueError when plan invariants are violated."""
   220→    if not isinstance(plan.get("version"), int):
   221→        raise ValueError("plan.version must be an int")
   222→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   223→        raise ValueError("plan.queue_order must be a list")
   224→
   225→    # No ID should appear in both queue_order and skipped
   226→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   227→    overlap = set(plan["queue_order"]) & skipped_ids
   228→    if overlap:
   229→        raise ValueError(
   230→            f"IDs cannot appear in both queue_order and skipped: {sorted(overlap)}"
   231→        )
   232→
   233→    # Validate skip entry kinds
   234→    for fid, entry in plan.get("skipped", {}).items():
   235→        kind = entry.get("kind")
   236→        if kind not in VALID_SKIP_KINDS:
   237→            raise ValueError(
   238→                f"Invalid skip kind {kind!r} for {fid}; must be one of {sorted(VALID_SKIP_KINDS)}"
   239→            )
   240→
   241→
   242→__all__ = [
   243→    "ActionStep",
   244→    "EPIC_PREFIX",
   245→    "EpicTriageMeta",
   246→    "ExecutionLogEntry",
   247→    "PLAN_VERSION",
   248→    "Cluster",
   249→    "CommitRecord",
   250→    "ItemOverride",
   251→    "LastTriageSnapshot",
   252→    "PlanModel",
   253→    "PlanStartScores",
   254→    "SkipEntry",
   255→    "SupersededEntry",
   256→    "TriageStagePayload",
   257→    "VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS",
   258→    "VALID_SKIP_KINDS",
   259→    "empty_plan",
   260→    "ensure_plan_defaults",
   261→    "triage_clusters",
   262→    "validate_plan",
   263→]
   264→
```

    15→_PREAMBLE = """\
    16→You are a triage subagent with full codebase access and the desloppify CLI.
    17→Your job is to complete the **{stage}** stage of triage planning.
    18→
    19→Repo root: {repo_root}
    20→
    21→## Standards
    22→
    23→You are expected to produce **exceptional** work. The output of this triage becomes the
    24→actual plan that an executor follows — if you are lazy, vague, or sloppy, real work gets
    25→wasted. Concretely:
    26→
    27→- **Read the actual source code.** Every opinion you form must come from reading the file,
    28→  not from reading the issue title. Issues frequently exaggerate, miscount, or describe
    29→  code that has already been fixed. Trust nothing until you verify it.
    30→- **Have specific opinions.** "This seems like it could be an issue" is worthless. "This is
    31→  a false positive because line 47 already uses the pattern the issue suggests" is useful.
    32→- **Do the hard thinking.** If two issues seem related, figure out WHY. If something should
    33→  be skipped, explain the specific reason for THIS issue, not a generic category.
    34→- **Don't take shortcuts.** Reading 5 files and extrapolating to 30 is lazy. Read all 30.
    35→  If you have too many, use subagents to parallelize — don't skip.
    36→
    37→Use the desloppify CLI to record your work. Every command you run mutates plan.json directly.
    38→The orchestrator will review your work and confirm the stage after you record it.
    39→
    40→**CRITICAL: Only run commands for YOUR stage ({stage}).** Do NOT re-run earlier stages
    41→(e.g., do not run `--stage observe` if you are the organize subagent). Earlier stages
    42→are already confirmed. Re-running them will corrupt the plan state.

    79→def _observe_instructions() -> str:
    80→    return """\
    81→## OBSERVE Stage Instructions
    82→
    83→Your task: verify every open review issue against the actual source code.
    84→
    85→**The review system has a high false-positive rate.** Issues frequently:
    86→- Claim "12 unsafe casts" when there are actually 2
    87→- Describe code that was already refactored
    88→- Propose over-engineering that would make things worse
    89→- Count props/returns/args wrong
    90→
    91→Your job is to catch these. An observe report that just restates issue titles is **worthless**.
    92→The value you add is reading the actual code and forming an independent judgment.
    93→
    94→Do NOT analyze themes, strategy, or relationships between issues. That's the next stage (reflect).
    95→Just verify: is each issue real?
    96→
    97→**CRITICAL: You must cite specific issue IDs (hash prefixes like [abcd1234]) in your report.**
    98→The confirmation gate requires citing at least 10% of issues (or 5, whichever is smaller).
    99→
   100→**USE SUBAGENTS to parallelize this work.** Launch parallel subagents — one per dimension
   101→group — to investigate concurrently. Each subagent MUST:
   102→- Open and read the actual source file for EVERY assigned issue
   103→- Verify specific claims: count the actual casts, props, returns, line count
   104→- Check if the suggested fix already exists (common false positive)
   105→- Report a clear verdict per issue: genuine / false positive / exaggerated / over-engineering
   106→
   107→Example subagent split for 90 issues across 17 dimensions:
   108→- Subagent 1: architecture + organization (cross_module_architecture, package_organization, high_level_elegance)
   109→- Subagent 2: abstraction + design (abstraction_fitness, design_coherence, mid_level_elegance)
   110→- Subagent 3: duplicates + contracts (contract_coherence, api_surface_coherence, low_level_elegance)
   111→- Subagent 4: migrations + debt + conventions (incomplete_migration, ai_generated_debt, convention_outlier, naming_quality)
   112→- Subagent 5: type safety + errors + tests (type_safety, error_consistency, test_strategy, initialization_coupling, dependency_health)
   113→
   114→**What a GOOD observe report looks like:**
   115→- "[34580232] taskType is plain string — FALSE POSITIVE. Uses branded string union KnownTaskType
   116→  with ~25 literals in src/types/database.ts line 50. The issue describes code that doesn't exist."
   117→- "[b634fc71] useGenerationsPaneController returns 60+ values — GENUINE. Confirmed 65 properties
   118→  at lines 217-282. Mixes pane lifecycle, filters, gallery data, interaction, and navigation."
   119→
   120→**What a LAZY observe report looks like (will be rejected):**
   121→- "There are several convention issues that should be addressed"
   122→- "The type safety dimension has some genuine concerns"
   123→- Listing issue titles without any verification or independent analysis
   124→
   125→**Your report must include for EVERY issue:**
   126→1. The hash prefix
   127→2. Your verdict (genuine / false positive / exaggerated / over-engineering)
   128→3. The specific evidence (what you found when you read the code)
   129→
   130→When done, run:
   131→```
   132→desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report "<your analysis with [hash] citations>"
   133→```
   134→"""
   135→
   136→
   137→def _reflect_instructions() -> str:
   138→    return """\
   139→## REFLECT Stage Instructions
   140→
   141→Your task: using the verdicts from observe, design the cluster structure.
   142→
   143→**A strategy is NOT a restatement of observe.** Observe says "here's what I found." Reflect
   144→says "here's what we should DO about it, and here's what we should NOT do, and here's WHY."
   145→
   146→### What you must do:
   147→
   148→1. **Filter:** which issues are genuine (from observe verdicts)?
   149→2. **Map:** for each genuine issue, what file/directory does it touch?
   150→3. **Group:** which issues share files or directories? These become clusters.
   151→4. **Skip:** which issues should be skipped? (with per-issue justification — "low priority" is
   152→   not a justification; "the fix would add a 50-line abstraction to save 3 lines of duplication" is)
   153→5. **Order:** which clusters depend on others? What's the execution sequence?
   154→6. **Check recurring patterns** — compare current issues against resolved history. If the same
   155→   dimension keeps producing issues, that's a root cause that needs addressing, not just
   156→   another round of fixes.
   157→
   158→### Your report MUST include a concrete cluster blueprint
   159→
   160→This blueprint is what the organize stage will execute. Be specific:
   161→```
   162→Cluster "media-lightbox-hooks": issues X, Y, Z (all in src/domains/media-lightbox/)
   163→Cluster "task-typing": issues A, B (both touch src/types/database.ts)
   164→Skip: issue W (false positive per observe), issue V (over-engineering — fix adds 50 lines for 3 lines saved)
   165→```
   166→
   167→### What a LAZY reflect looks like (will be rejected):
   168→- Restating observe findings in slightly different words
   169→- "We should prioritize high-impact items and defer low-priority ones"
   170→- A bulleted list of dimensions without any strategic thinking
   171→- Ignoring recurring patterns
   172→- No cluster blueprint (just vague grouping ideas)
   173→
   174→### What a GOOD reflect looks like:
   175→- "50% false positive rate. Of 34 issues, 17 are genuine. 10 of those are batch-scriptable
   176→  convention fixes (zero risk, 30 min) — cluster 'convention-batch'. The remaining 7 split into
   177→  3 clusters by file proximity: 'media-lightbox-hooks' (issues X,Y,Z — all in src/domains/media-lightbox/),
   178→  'timeline-cleanup' (issues A,B,C — touching Timeline components), 'task-typing' (issues D,E).
   179→  Skip: issue W (false positive), issue V (over-engineering).
   180→  design_coherence recurs (2 resolved, 5 open) but only 1 of the 5 actually warrants work."
   181→
   182→When done, run:
   183→```
   184→desloppify plan triage --stage reflect --report "<your strategy with cluster blueprint>" --attestation "<80+ chars mentioning dimensions or recurring patterns>"
   185→```
   186→"""
   187→
   188→
   189→def _organize_instructions() -> str:
   190→    return """\
   191→## ORGANIZE Stage Instructions
   192→
   193→Your task: execute the cluster blueprint from the reflect stage.
   194→
   195→The reflect report contains a specific plan: which clusters to create, which issues go
   196→where, what to skip. Build it using the CLI. If something doesn't work as planned
   197→(issue hash doesn't match, file proximity doesn't hold), adjust and document why.
   198→
   199→This stage should be largely mechanical. If you find yourself making major strategic
   200→decisions, something went wrong in reflect — the strategy should already be decided.
   201→
   202→### Process
   203→
   204→1. Review the reflect report's cluster blueprint (provided below)
   205→2. **Skip false positives and over-engineering** identified in observe/reflect. Every skip needs a
   206→   per-issue justification — not "low priority" but "false positive: the code at line 47
   207→   already uses named constants, contradicting the issue's claim":
   208→   ```
   209→   desloppify plan skip --permanent <pattern> --note "<specific per-issue reason>" --attest "<attestation>"
   210→   ```
   211→3. Create clusters as specified in the blueprint:
   212→   `desloppify plan cluster create <name> --description "..."`
   213→4. Add issues: `desloppify plan cluster add <name> <patterns...>`
   214→5. Add steps that consolidate: one step per file or logical change, NOT one step per issue
   215→6. Set `--effort` on each step individually (trivial/small/medium/large)
   216→7. Set `--depends-on` when clusters touch overlapping files
   217→
   218→### Quality gates (the confirmation will check these)
   219→
   220→Before recording, verify:
   221→- [ ] Every cluster name describes an area or specific change, not a problem type
   222→- [ ] No cluster has issues from 5+ unrelated directories (theme-group smell)
   223→- [ ] Step count < issue count (consolidation happened)
   224→- [ ] Every skip has a specific per-issue reason (not "low priority")
   225→- [ ] Overlapping clusters have --depends-on set
   226→- [ ] Cluster descriptions describe the WORK, not the PROBLEMS
   227→
   228→Every review issue must end up in a cluster OR be skipped.
   229→
   230→When done, run:
   231→```
   232→desloppify plan triage --stage organize --report "<summary of priorities and organization>" --attestation "<80+ chars mentioning cluster names>"
   233→```
   234→"""
   235→
   236→
   237→def _enrich_instructions() -> str:
   238→    return """\
   239→## ENRICH Stage Instructions
   240→
   241→Your task: make EVERY step executor-ready. The test: could a developer who has never seen
   242→this codebase read your step detail and make the change without asking a single question?
   243→
   244→If the answer is "they'd need to figure out which file" or "they'd need to understand the
   245→context" — your step is not ready. Be specific enough that the work is mechanical.
   246→
   247→### Requirements (ALL BLOCKING — confirmation will reject if not met)
   248→
   249→1. Every step MUST have `--detail` with 80+ chars INCLUDING at least one file path (src/... or supabase/...)
   250→2. Every step MUST have `--issue-refs` linking it to specific review issue hash(es)
   251→3. Every step MUST have `--effort` tag (trivial/small/medium/large) — set INDIVIDUALLY, not bulk
   252→4. File paths in detail MUST exist on disk (validator checks this)
   253→5. No step may reference a skipped/wontfixed issue in its issue_refs
   254→
   255→### How to enrich
   256→
   257→**USE SUBAGENTS — one per cluster.** Each subagent MUST:
   258→
   259→1. Run `desloppify plan cluster show <name>` to get current steps and issue list
   260→2. **Read the actual source file for every step** — not just the issue description.
   261→   The issue says what's wrong; you need to see the code to say what to DO.
   262→3. Write detail that includes: the file path, the specific location (line range or
   263→   function name), and the exact change to make
   264→4. Set effort based on the ACTUAL complexity you see in the code, not a guess
   265→
   266→### Common lazy patterns to avoid
   267→
   268→**Copying the issue description as step detail.** The issue says "useGenerationsPaneController
   269→returns 60+ values mixing concerns." That's a PROBLEM description. The step detail should say
   270→"In src/shared/components/GenerationsPane/hooks/useGenerationsPaneController.ts (283 lines),
   271→extract lines 45-89 (filter state: activeFilter, setActiveFilter, filterOptions, applyFilter)
   272→into a new useGenerationFilters hook. The controller imports and spreads the sub-hook's return."
   273→
   274→**Vague action verbs.** "Refactor", "clean up", "improve", "fix" are not actions.
   275→"Extract lines 45-89 into useGenerationFilters", "delete lines 12-18", "rename the file
   276→from X to Y and update the 3 imports in A.tsx, B.tsx, C.tsx" are actions.
   277→
   278→**Guessing file paths.** If you write `src/shared/lib/jsonNarrowing.ts` and it doesn't exist,
   279→confirmation will block. READ the file system. Only reference files you've verified exist.
   280→
   281→**Bulk effort tags.** Don't mark everything "small". A file rename with 2 imports is "trivial".
   282→Decomposing a 400-line hook into 3 sub-hooks is "medium" or "large". Think about each one.
   283→
   284→### Examples
   285→
   286→**GOOD step detail:**
   287→```
   288→--detail "In src/shared/hooks/billing/useAutoTopup.ts lines 118-129, add onMutate handler
   289→to capture previous queryClient state before optimistic update. In onError callback, restore
   290→previous state and change showToast from false to true."
   291→--issue-refs 79baeebf --effort small
   292→```
   293→
   294→**BAD step detail (will be rejected):**
   295→```
   296→--detail "Fix silent error swallowing"  # No file, no location, no action
   297→--detail "Decompose god-hooks"  # What file? What hooks? Into what?
   298→--detail "Address the issues identified in the observe stage"  # This says nothing
   299→```
   300→
   301→### Do NOT mark steps as done
   302→
   303→Use `--update-step N` to add detail, effort, and issue-refs.
   304→Do NOT use `--done-step` — steps are only marked done when actual code changes are made.
   305→
   306→### File path rules
   307→
   308→Only reference files that exist RIGHT NOW. Do not reference files that a step will create
   309→(e.g., a new shared module) or rename targets (the new filename after a rename). Reference
   310→the current source file and describe what will change. The path validator will block
   311→confirmation if paths don't exist on disk.
   312→
   313→When done, run:
   314→```
   315→desloppify plan triage --stage enrich --report "<enrichment summary>" --attestation "<80+ chars mentioning cluster names>"
   316→```
   317→"""
   318→
   319→def _sense_check_instructions() -> str:
   320→    return """\
   321→## SENSE-CHECK Stage Instructions
   322→
   323→This stage is handled by two parallel subagents. If you are being run as a
   324→single-subprocess fallback, perform BOTH the content and structure checks below.
   325→
   326→### Content Check (per cluster)
   327→For EVERY step in every cluster, read the actual source file and verify:
   328→1. LINE NUMBERS: Does the code at the claimed lines match the step description?
   329→2. NAMES: Do function/variable/type names in the step exist in the file?
   330→3. COUNTS: Are counts ("update the 3 imports") accurate?
   331→4. STALENESS: Is the problem still present, or already fixed?
   332→5. VAGUENESS: Could a developer with zero context execute this step?
   333→6. EFFORT TAGS: Does the tag match actual scope?
   334→7. DUPLICATES: Flag steps that duplicate work in another cluster.
   335→8. OVER-ENGINEERING: Would this change make the codebase *worse*? Flag steps that:
   336→   - Add abstractions, wrappers, or indirection for a one-time operation
   337→   - Introduce unnecessary config/feature-flags/generalization
   338→   - Make simple code harder to read for marginal benefit
   339→   - Gold-plate beyond what the issue actually requires
   340→   - Trade one smell for a worse one (e.g. fix duplication by adding a fragile base class)
   341→   Remove or simplify over-engineered steps. If the whole cluster is net-negative, say so.
   342→
   343→Fix with: `desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail "..." --effort <tag>`
   344→
   345→### Structure Check (global)
   346→Build a file-touch graph and check:
   347→1. SHARED FILES: Two clusters touching same file without --depends-on → add dependency
   348→2. MISSING CASCADE: Rename/remove without importer updates → add cascade step
   349→3. CIRCULAR DEPS: Flag cycles, don't add them
   350→
   351→Fix with: `desloppify plan cluster update <name> --depends-on <other>`
   352→Fix with: `desloppify plan cluster update <name> --add-step "..." --detail "..." --effort trivial --issue-refs <hash>`
   353→
   354→When done, run:
   355→```
   356→desloppify plan triage --stage sense-check --report "<findings summary>"
   357→```
   358→"""
   359→
   360→
   361→_STAGE_INSTRUCTIONS = {
   362→    "observe": _observe_instructions,
   363→    "reflect": _reflect_instructions,
   364→    "organize": _organize_instructions,
   365→    "enrich": _enrich_instructions,
   366→    "sense-check": _sense_check_instructions,
   367→}
   368→
   369→
   370→def build_stage_prompt(
   371→    stage: str,
   372→    triage_input: TriageInput,
   373→    prior_reports: dict[str, str],
   374→    *,
   375→    repo_root: Path,
   376→) -> str:
   377→    """Build a complete subagent prompt for a triage stage."""
   378→    parts: list[str] = []
   379→
   380→    # Preamble
   381→    parts.append(_PREAMBLE.format(stage=stage.upper(), repo_root=repo_root))
   382→
   383→    # Issue data
   384→    issue_data = build_triage_prompt(triage_input)
   385→    parts.append("## Issue Data\n\n" + issue_data)
   386→
   387→    # Prior stage reports
   388→    if prior_reports:
   389→        parts.append("## Prior Stage Reports\n")
   390→        for prior_stage, report in prior_reports.items():
   391→            parts.append(f"### {prior_stage.upper()} Report\n{report}\n")
   392→
   393→    # Stage-specific instructions
   394→    instruction_fn = _STAGE_INSTRUCTIONS.get(stage)
   395→    if instruction_fn:
   396→        parts.append(instruction_fn())
   397→
   398→    # CLI reference
   399→    parts.append(_CLI_REFERENCE)
   400→
   401→    # Validation requirements
   402→    parts.append(_validation_requirements(stage))
   403→
   404→    return "\n\n".join(parts)
   405→
   406→
   407→def cmd_stage_prompt(
   408→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   409→    *,
   410→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   411→) -> None:
   412→    """Print the current prompt for a triage stage, built from live plan data."""
   413→    stage = args.stage_prompt
   414→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   415→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   416→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
   417→    state = runtime.state
   418→    si = resolved_services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
   419→    repo_root = get_project_root()
   420→
   421→    # Extract real prior reports from plan.json
   422→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   423→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   424→    prior_reports: dict[str, str] = {}
   425→    for prior_stage in _STAGES:
   426→        if prior_stage == stage:
   427→            break
   428→        report = stages.get(prior_stage, {}).get("report", "")
   429→        if report:
   430→            prior_reports[prior_stage] = report
   431→
   432→    prompt = build_stage_prompt(stage, si, prior_reports, repo_root=repo_root)
   433→    print(prompt)
   434→
   435→
   436→def _validation_requirements(stage: str) -> str:
   437→    """What must be true for the stage to pass validation."""
   438→    if stage == "observe":
   439→        return (
   440→            "## Validation Requirements\n"
   441→            "- Stage must be recorded with a 100+ char report\n"
   442→            "- Report must cite at least 10% of issue IDs (or 5, whichever is smaller)\n"
   443→            "- Stage must be confirmed with an 80+ char attestation mentioning dimension names\n"
   444→        )
   445→    if stage == "reflect":
   446→        return (
   447→            "## Validation Requirements\n"
   448→            "- Stage must be recorded with a 100+ char report\n"
   449→            "- Report must mention recurring dimension names (if any exist)\n"
   450→            "- Stage must be confirmed with an 80+ char attestation\n"
   451→        )
   452→    if stage == "organize":
   453→        return (
   454→            "## Validation Requirements\n"
   455→            "- At least one manual cluster with issues must exist\n"
   456→            "- All manual clusters must have description and action steps\n"
   457→            "- All review issues must be in a cluster or skipped\n"
   458→            "- Overlapping clusters must have --depends-on relationships\n"
   459→            "- Cluster descriptions must reflect current issues (not stale/skipped ones)\n"
   460→            "- Clusters must group by file/area proximity, not by dimension or theme\n"
   461→            "- A cluster whose issues span 5+ unrelated directories will be flagged\n"
   462→            "- Step count should be less than issue count (consolidate shared-file changes)\n"
   463→            "- Stage must be recorded and confirmed\n"
   464→        )
   465→    if stage == "enrich":
   466→        return (
   467→            "## Validation Requirements (ALL BLOCKING — not advisory)\n"
   468→            "- Every step needs --detail with 80+ chars INCLUDING a file path\n"
   469→            "- Every step needs --issue-refs linking to review issue(s)\n"
   470→            "- Every step needs --effort tag (trivial/small/medium/large)\n"
   471→            "- No bad file paths in step details (must exist on disk)\n"
   472→            "- No steps referencing skipped/wontfixed issues\n"
   473→            "- Stage must be recorded and confirmed\n"
   474→        )
   475→    if stage == "sense-check":
   476→        return (
   477→            "## Validation Requirements (ALL BLOCKING)\n"
   478→            "- Re-runs ALL enrich-level checks (detail, issue_refs, effort, paths, vagueness)\n"
   479→            "- Stage must be recorded with a 100+ char report\n"
   480→            "- Stage must be confirmed with an 80+ char attestation mentioning cluster names\n"
   481→        )
   482→    return ""
   483→
   484→
   485→def _observe_batch_instructions(issue_count: int, total_batches: int) -> str:
   486→    return f"""\
   487→## OBSERVE Batch Instructions
   488→
   489→You are one of {total_batches} parallel observe batches. Your task: verify every issue
   490→assigned to you against the actual source code.
   491→
   492→**The review system has a high false-positive rate.** Issues frequently:
   493→- Claim "12 unsafe casts" when there are actually 2
   494→- Describe code that was already refactored
   495→- Propose over-engineering that would make things worse
   496→- Count props/returns/args wrong
   497→
   498→Your job is to catch these. A report that just restates issue titles is **worthless**.
   499→The value you add is reading the actual code and forming an independent judgment.
   500→
   501→Do NOT analyze themes, strategy, or relationships between issues. Just verify: is each issue real?
   502→
   503→**For EVERY issue you must:**
   504→- Open and read the actual source file
   505→- Verify specific claims: count the actual casts, props, returns, line count
   506→- Check if the suggested fix already exists (common false positive)
   507→- Report a clear verdict: genuine / false positive / exaggerated / over-engineering
   508→
   509→**What a GOOD report looks like:**
   510→- "[34580232] taskType is plain string — FALSE POSITIVE. Uses branded string union KnownTaskType
   511→  with ~25 literals in src/types/database.ts line 50. The issue describes code that doesn't exist."
   512→- "[b634fc71] useGenerationsPaneController returns 60+ values — GENUINE. Confirmed 65 properties
   513→  at lines 217-282. Mixes pane lifecycle, filters, gallery data, interaction, and navigation."
   514→
   515→**What a LAZY report looks like (will be rejected):**
   516→- "There are several convention issues that should be addressed"
   517→- "The type safety dimension has some genuine concerns"
   518→- Listing issue titles without any verification or independent analysis
   519→
   520→**Your report must include for EVERY issue ({issue_count} total):**
   521→1. The hash prefix in brackets
   522→2. Your verdict (genuine / false positive / exaggerated / over-engineering)
   523→3. The specific evidence (what you found when you read the code)
   524→
   525→## IMPORTANT: Output Rules
   526→
   527→**Do NOT run any `desloppify` commands.** Do NOT run `desloppify plan triage --stage observe`.
   528→You are a parallel batch — the orchestrator will merge all batch outputs and record the stage.
   529→
   530→**Write your analysis as plain text only.** Format:
   531→```
   532→[hash_prefix] VERDICT — evidence
   533→```
   534→"""
   535→
   536→
   537→def build_observe_batch_prompt(
   538→    batch_index: int,
   539→    total_batches: int,
   540→    dimension_group: list[str],
   541→    issues_subset: dict[str, dict],
   542→    *,
   543→    repo_root: Path,
   544→) -> str:
   545→    """Build a scoped observe prompt for a single dimension-group batch.
   546→
   547→    Unlike build_stage_prompt(), this produces a prompt for observe only,
   548→    scoped to a subset of issues. The batch subprocess writes analysis to
   549→    stdout — it does NOT run ``desloppify plan triage --stage observe``.
   550→    The orchestrator merges batch outputs and records observe once.
   551→    """
   552→    parts: list[str] = []
   553→
   554→    # Batch context header
   555→    parts.append(
   556→        f"You are observe batch {batch_index}/{total_batches}.\n"
   557→        f"Dimensions assigned to you: {', '.join(dimension_group)}\n"
   558→        f"Total issues in this batch: {len(issues_subset)}\n\n"
   559→        f"Repo root: {repo_root}"
   560→    )
   561→
   562→    # Issue data — inline the subset directly
   563→    parts.append("## Issues to Verify\n")
   564→    for fid, f in sorted(issues_subset.items()):
   565→        detail = f.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(f.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   566→        dim = detail.get("dimension", "unknown")
   567→        title = f.get("title", fid)
   568→        file_path = detail.get("file_path", "")
   569→        description = detail.get("description", f.get("description", ""))
   570→        line = f"- [{fid[:8]}] ({dim}) **{title}**"
   571→        if file_path:
   572→            line += f" — `{file_path}`"
   573→        if description:
   574→            line += f"\n  {description[:300]}"
   575→        parts.append(line)
   576→
   577→    # Batch-specific observe instructions (no subagent/CLI references)
   578→    parts.append(_observe_batch_instructions(len(issues_subset), total_batches))
   579→
   580→    return "\n\n".join(parts)
   581→
   582→
   583→def build_sense_check_content_prompt(
   584→    *,
   585→    cluster_name: str,
   586→    plan: dict,
   587→    repo_root: Path,
   588→) -> str:
   589→    """Build a content-verification prompt for a single cluster."""
   590→    cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(cluster_name, {})
   591→    steps = cluster.get("action_steps", [])
   592→    issue_ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   593→
   594→    parts: list[str] = []
   595→    parts.append(
   596→        f"You are sense-checking cluster `{cluster_name}` "
   597→        f"({len(steps)} steps, {len(issue_ids)} issues).\n"
   598→        f"Repo root: {repo_root}"
   599→    )
   600→
   601→    parts.append(
   602→        "## Your job\n"
   603→        "For EVERY step in this cluster, read the actual source file and verify\n"
   604→        "every factual claim. Then fix anything wrong or vague.\n"
   605→    )
   606→
   607→    parts.append(
   608→        "## What to check and fix\n"
   609→        "1. LINE NUMBERS: Does the code at the claimed lines match what the step describes?\n"
   610→        "   Fix: update the line range to match current file state.\n"
   611→        "2. NAMES: Do the function/variable/type names in the step exist in the file?\n"
   612→        "   Fix: correct the names.\n"
   613→        "3. COUNTS: \"Update the 3 imports\" — are there actually 3? Or 5?\n"
   614→        "   Fix: correct the count.\n"
   615→        "4. STALENESS: Is the problem the issue describes still present in the code?\n"
   616→        "   If already fixed, note in your report.\n"
   617→        "5. VAGUENESS: Could a developer with zero context execute this step without\n"
   618→        "   asking a single question? If not:\n"
   619→        "   - Replace \"refactor X\" with the specific transformation\n"
   620→        "   - Replace \"update imports\" with the specific file list\n"
   621→        "   - Replace \"extract into new hook\" with the filename, function signature, return type\n"
   622→        "6. EFFORT TAGS: Does the tag match the actual scope? A one-line rename is \"trivial\",\n"
   623→        "   not \"small\". Decomposing a 400-line file is \"large\", not \"medium\".\n"
   624→        "7. DUPLICATES: If you notice this step does the same thing as a step in another\n"
   625→        "   cluster, note it in your report.\n"
   626→        "8. OVER-ENGINEERING: Would this change make the codebase *worse*? Flag steps that:\n"
   627→        "   - Add abstractions, wrappers, or indirection for a one-time operation\n"
   628→        "   - Introduce unnecessary config, feature flags, or generalization\n"
   629→        "   - Make simple code harder to read for marginal benefit\n"
   630→        "   - Gold-plate beyond what the issue actually requires\n"
   631→        "   - Trade one smell for a worse one (e.g. fix duplication by adding a fragile base class)\n"
   632→        "   If a step is net-negative, recommend removing it or simplifying the approach.\n"
   633→        "   If the entire cluster is net-negative, say so clearly in your report.\n"
   634→    )
   635→
   636→    parts.append(
   637→        "## How to fix\n"
   638→        f"desloppify plan cluster update {cluster_name} "
   639→        "--update-step N --detail \"corrected...\" --effort <tag>\n"
   640→    )
   641→
   642→    parts.append(
   643→        "## What NOT to do\n"
   644→        "- Do NOT reorder steps (the structure subagent handles that)\n"
   645→        "- Do NOT add --depends-on (the structure subagent handles that)\n"
   646→        "- Do NOT add new steps for missing cascade updates (the structure subagent handles that)\n"
   647→        "- Do NOT run any other desloppify commands\n"
   648→    )
   649→
   650→    # Include cluster steps
   651→    parts.append("## Current Steps\n")
   652→    for i, step in enumerate(steps, 1):
   653→        title = step.get("title", str(step)) if isinstance(step, dict) else str(step)
   654→        detail = step.get("detail", "") if isinstance(step, dict) else ""
   655→        effort = step.get("effort", "") if isinstance(step, dict) else ""
   656→        refs = step.get("issue_refs", []) if isinstance(step, dict) else []
   657→        line = f"{i}. **{title}**"
   658→        if effort:
   659→            line += f" [{effort}]"
   660→        if refs:
   661→            line += f" (refs: {', '.join(refs[:3])})"
   662→        if detail:
   663→            line += f"\n   {detail[:300]}"
   664→        parts.append(line)
   665→
   666→    parts.append(
   667→        "\n## Output\n"
   668→        "Write a plain-text report of your findings. The orchestrator records the stage."
   669→    )
   670→
   671→    return "\n\n".join(parts)
   672→
   673→
   674→def build_sense_check_structure_prompt(
   675→    *,
   676→    plan: dict,
   677→    repo_root: Path,
   678→) -> str:
   679→    """Build a structure-verification prompt for cross-cluster dependency checking."""
   680→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   681→
   682→    parts: list[str] = []
   683→    parts.append(
   684→        "You are checking cross-cluster dependencies for the entire triage plan.\n"
   685→        f"Repo root: {repo_root}"
   686→    )
   687→
   688→    parts.append(
   689→        "## Your job\n"
   690→        "Build a file-touch graph: for each cluster, which files do its steps reference?\n"
   691→        "Then check for unsafe relationships between clusters.\n"
   692→    )
   693→
   694→    parts.append(
   695→        "## What to check and fix\n"
   696→        "1. SHARED FILES: If cluster A and cluster B both have steps touching the same file,\n"
   697→        "   and neither depends on the other → add a dependency.\n"
   698→        "   Fix: desloppify plan cluster update {later_cluster} --depends-on {earlier_cluster}\n"
   699→        "2. MISSING CASCADE: If a step renames/removes a function or export, check whether\n"
   700→        "   any other file imports it. If those importers aren't covered by any step in any\n"
   701→        "   cluster → add a cascade step.\n"
   702→        "   Fix: desloppify plan cluster update {cluster} --add-step \"Update importers of {name}\"\n"
   703→        "        --detail \"Files importing {old}: {list}. Update import to {new}.\"\n"
   704→        "        --effort trivial --issue-refs {hash}\n"
   705→        "3. CIRCULAR DEPS: If adding a dependency would create a cycle, flag it in your report\n"
   706→        "   instead of adding it.\n"
   707→    )
   708→
   709→    parts.append(
   710→        "## What NOT to do\n"
   711→        "- Do NOT modify step detail text (the content subagent handles that)\n"
   712→        "- Do NOT change effort tags (the content subagent handles that)\n"
   713→        "- Do NOT remove steps or deduplicate (the content subagent handles that)\n"
   714→        "- Do NOT run any other desloppify commands besides cluster update --depends-on and --add-step\n"
   715→    )
   716→
   717→    # Include all clusters with their steps and dependencies
   718→    parts.append("## Clusters\n")
   719→    for name, c in sorted(clusters.items()):
   720→        if c.get("auto"):
   721→            continue
   722→        steps = c.get("action_steps", [])
   723→        deps = c.get("depends_on_clusters", [])
   724→        issues = c.get("issue_ids", [])
   725→        header = f"### {name} ({len(steps)} steps, {len(issues)} issues)"
   726→        if deps:
   727→            header += f"\n  depends_on: {', '.join(deps)}"
   728→        parts.append(header)
   729→        for i, step in enumerate(steps, 1):
   730→            title = step.get("title", str(step)) if isinstance(step, dict) else str(step)
   731→            detail = step.get("detail", "") if isinstance(step, dict) else ""
   732→            line = f"  {i}. {title}"
   733→            if detail:
   734→                line += f"\n     {detail[:200]}"
   735→            parts.append(line)
   736→
   737→    parts.append(
   738→        "\n## Output\n"
   739→        "Write a plain-text report of your findings. The orchestrator records the stage."
   740→    )
   741→
   742→    return "\n\n".join(parts)
   743→
   744→
   745→__all__ = [
   746→    "build_observe_batch_prompt",
   747→    "build_sense_check_content_prompt",
   748→    "build_sense_check_structure_prompt",
   749→    "build_stage_prompt",
   750→    "cmd_stage_prompt",
   751→]
   752→
```

1→"""Post-subagent validation and auto-attestation for triage runners."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from pathlib import Path
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine._plan.epic_triage_prompt import TriageInput
     8→
     9→from ..helpers import manual_clusters_with_issues, observe_dimension_breakdown
    10→from ..stage_helpers import _unclustered_review_issues, _unenriched_clusters
    11→from .._stage_validation import (
    12→    _cluster_file_overlaps,
    13→    _clusters_with_directory_scatter,
    14→    _clusters_with_high_step_ratio,
    15→    _shallow_steps,
    16→    _steps_missing_issue_refs,
    17→    _steps_with_bad_paths,
    18→    _steps_with_vague_detail,
    19→    _steps_without_effort,
    20→)
    21→
    22→
    23→def validate_stage(
    24→    stage: str,
    25→    plan: dict,
    26→    state: dict,
    27→    repo_root: Path,
    28→    *,
    29→    triage_input: TriageInput | None = None,
    30→) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    31→    """Check subagent completed stage correctly. Returns (ok, error_msg)."""
    32→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    33→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
    34→
    35→    if stage == "observe":
    36→        if "observe" not in stages:
    37→            return False, "Observe stage not recorded."
    38→        report = stages["observe"].get("report", "")
    39→        if len(report) < 100:
    40→            return False, f"Observe report too short ({len(report)} chars, need 100+)."
    41→        # Check citation ratio — must reference specific issues
    42→        cited = stages["observe"].get("cited_ids", [])
    43→        issue_count = stages["observe"].get("issue_count", 0)
    44→        if issue_count > 0:
    45→            min_citations = min(5, max(1, issue_count // 10))
    46→            if len(cited) < min_citations:
    47→                return False, (
    48→                    f"Observe report cites only {len(cited)} issue(s) "
    49→                    f"(need {min_citations}+). Reference specific issue "
    50→                    f"hashes to prove you read them."
    51→                )
    52→        return True, ""
    53→
    54→    if stage == "reflect":
    55→        if "reflect" not in stages:
    56→            return False, "Reflect stage not recorded."
    57→        report = stages["reflect"].get("report", "")
    58→        if len(report) < 100:
    59→            return False, f"Reflect report too short ({len(report)} chars, need 100+)."
    60→        return True, ""
    61→
    62→    if stage == "organize":
    63→        if "organize" not in stages:
    64→            return False, "Organize stage not recorded."
    65→        manual = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
    66→        if not manual:
    67→            return False, "No manual clusters with issues exist."
    68→        gaps = _unenriched_clusters(plan)
    69→        if gaps:
    70→            names = ", ".join(n for n, _ in gaps)
    71→            return False, f"Unenriched clusters: {names}"
    72→        unclustered = _unclustered_review_issues(plan, state)
    73→        if unclustered:
    74→            return False, f"{len(unclustered)} review issue(s) not in any cluster."
    75→        # Advisory warnings (non-blocking but informational)
    76→        warnings: list[str] = []
    77→        overlaps = _cluster_file_overlaps(plan)
    78→        if overlaps:
    79→            warnings.append(f"{len(overlaps)} cluster pair(s) share files without dependencies")
    80→        scattered = _clusters_with_directory_scatter(plan)
    81→        if scattered:
    82→            names = ", ".join(n for n, _, _ in scattered)
    83→            warnings.append(f"Theme-grouped clusters (5+ dirs): {names}")
    84→        high_ratio = _clusters_with_high_step_ratio(plan)
    85→        if high_ratio:
    86→            names = ", ".join(n for n, _, _, _ in high_ratio)
    87→            warnings.append(f"1:1 step-to-issue ratio: {names}")
    88→        # Orphaned clusters (steps but no issues)
    89→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    90→        orphaned = [
    91→            n for n, c in clusters.items()
    92→            if not c.get("auto") and not c.get("issue_ids") and c.get("action_steps")
    93→        ]
    94→        if orphaned:
    95→            warnings.append(f"Orphaned clusters (steps, no issues): {', '.join(orphaned)}")
    96→        if warnings:
    97→            # Return success with advisory message
    98→            return True, "Advisory: " + "; ".join(warnings)
    99→        return True, ""
   100→
   101→    if stage == "enrich":
   102→        if "enrich" not in stages:
   103→            return False, "Enrich stage not recorded."
   104→        shallow = _shallow_steps(plan)
   105→        if shallow:
   106→            total = sum(n for _, n, _ in shallow)
   107→            return False, f"{total} step(s) still lack detail or issue_refs."
   108→        bad_paths = _steps_with_bad_paths(plan, repo_root)
   109→        if bad_paths:
   110→            total = sum(len(bp) for _, _, bp in bad_paths)
   111→            return False, f"{total} file path(s) in step details don't exist on disk."
   112→        # Effort tags are now blocking
   113→        untagged = _steps_without_effort(plan)
   114→        if untagged:
   115→            total = sum(n for _, n, _ in untagged)
   116→            return False, f"{total} step(s) have no effort tag (trivial/small/medium/large)."
   117→        # Issue refs are now blocking
   118→        no_refs = _steps_missing_issue_refs(plan)
   119→        if no_refs:
   120→            total = sum(n for _, n, _ in no_refs)
   121→            return False, f"{total} step(s) have no issue_refs for traceability."
   122→        # Vague detail is now blocking
   123→        vague = _steps_with_vague_detail(plan, repo_root)
   124→        if vague:
   125→            return False, (
   126→                f"{len(vague)} step(s) have vague detail (< 80 chars, no file paths). "
   127→                f"Executor-ready means: file path + specific instruction."
   128→            )
   129→        return True, ""
   130→
   131→    if stage == "sense-check":
   132→        if "sense-check" not in stages:
   133→            return False, "Sense-check stage not recorded."
   134→        report = stages["sense-check"].get("report", "")
   135→        if len(report) < 100:
   136→            return False, f"Sense-check report too short ({len(report)} chars, need 100+)."
   137→        # Re-run all enrich-level checks (subagents may have introduced issues)
   138→        shallow = _shallow_steps(plan)
   139→        if shallow:
   140→            total = sum(n for _, n, _ in shallow)
   141→            return False, f"{total} step(s) still lack detail or issue_refs after sense-check."
   142→        bad_paths = _steps_with_bad_paths(plan, repo_root)
   143→        if bad_paths:
   144→            total = sum(len(bp) for _, _, bp in bad_paths)
   145→            return False, f"{total} file path(s) don't exist on disk after sense-check."
   146→        untagged = _steps_without_effort(plan)
   147→        if untagged:
   148→            total = sum(n for _, n, _ in untagged)
   149→            return False, f"{total} step(s) have no effort tag after sense-check."
   150→        no_refs = _steps_missing_issue_refs(plan)
   151→        if no_refs:
   152→            total = sum(n for _, n, _ in no_refs)
   153→            return False, f"{total} step(s) have no issue_refs after sense-check."
   154→        vague = _steps_with_vague_detail(plan, repo_root)
   155→        if vague:
   156→            return False, f"{len(vague)} step(s) have vague detail after sense-check."
   157→        return True, ""
   158→
   159→    return False, f"Unknown stage: {stage}"
   160→
   161→
   162→def validate_completion(
   163→    plan: dict,
   164→    state: dict,
   165→    repo_root: Path,
   166→) -> tuple[bool, str]:
   167→    """Validate plan is ready for triage completion. Returns (ok, error_msg)."""
   168→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   169→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   170→
   171→    for required in ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "sense-check"):
   172→        if required not in stages:
   173→            return False, f"Stage {required} not recorded."
   174→        if not stages[required].get("confirmed_at"):
   175→            return False, f"Stage {required} not confirmed."
   176→
   177→    manual = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   178→    if not manual:
   179→        return False, "No manual clusters with issues."
   180→
   181→    gaps = _unenriched_clusters(plan)
   182→    if gaps:
   183→        return False, f"{len(gaps)} cluster(s) still need enrichment."
   184→
   185→    unclustered = _unclustered_review_issues(plan, state)
   186→    if unclustered:
   187→        return False, f"{len(unclustered)} review issue(s) not in any cluster."
   188→
   189→    # Quality warnings (non-blocking but logged)
   190→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   191→    # Check dependency ordering
   192→    for name, c in clusters.items():
   193→        deps = c.get("depends_on_clusters", [])
   194→        if name in deps:
   195→            return False, f"Cluster {name} depends on itself."
   196→
   197→    # Check for all-trivial clusters
   198→    all_trivial_clusters = []
   199→    total_steps = 0
   200→    for name, c in clusters.items():
   201→        if c.get("auto") or not c.get("issue_ids"):
   202→            continue
   203→        steps = c.get("action_steps") or []
   204→        total_steps += len(steps)
   205→        if steps and all(
   206→            isinstance(s, dict) and s.get("effort") == "trivial" for s in steps
   207→        ):
   208→            all_trivial_clusters.append(name)
   209→
   210→    return True, ""
   211→
   212→
   213→def build_auto_attestation(
   214→    stage: str,
   215→    plan: dict,
   216→    triage_input: TriageInput,
   217→) -> str:
   218→    """Generate valid 80+ char attestation referencing real dimensions/cluster names."""
   219→    if stage == "observe":
   220→        _by_dim, dim_names = observe_dimension_breakdown(triage_input)
   221→        top_dims = dim_names[:3]
   222→        dims_str = ", ".join(top_dims)
   223→        return (
   224→            f"I have thoroughly analysed {len(triage_input.open_issues)} issues "
   225→            f"across dimensions including {dims_str}, identifying themes, "
   226→            f"root causes, and contradictions across the codebase."
   227→        )
   228→
   229→    if stage == "reflect":
   230→        _by_dim, dim_names = observe_dimension_breakdown(triage_input)
   231→        top_dims = dim_names[:3]
   232→        dims_str = ", ".join(top_dims)
   233→        return (
   234→            f"My strategy accounts for {len(triage_input.open_issues)} issues "
   235→            f"across dimensions including {dims_str}, comparing against "
   236→            f"resolved history and forming priorities for execution."
   237→        )
   238→
   239→    if stage == "organize":
   240→        cluster_names = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   241→        names_str = ", ".join(cluster_names[:3])
   242→        return (
   243→            f"I have organized all review issues into clusters including "
   244→            f"{names_str}, with descriptions, action steps, and clear "
   245→            f"priority ordering based on root cause analysis."
   246→        )
   247→
   248→    if stage == "enrich":
   249→        cluster_names = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   250→        names_str = ", ".join(cluster_names[:3])
   251→        return (
   252→            f"Steps in clusters including {names_str} are executor-ready with "
   253→            f"detail, file paths, issue refs, and effort tags, verified "
   254→            f"against the actual codebase."
   255→        )
   256→
   257→    if stage == "sense-check":
   258→        cluster_names = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   259→        names_str = ", ".join(cluster_names[:3])
   260→        return (
   261→            f"Content and structure verified for clusters including {names_str}. "
   262→            f"All step details are factually accurate, cross-cluster dependencies "
   263→            f"are safe, and enrich-level checks pass."
   264→        )
   265→
   266→    return f"Stage {stage} completed with thorough analysis of all available data and verified against codebase."
   267→
   268→
   269→__all__ = [
   270→    "build_auto_attestation",
   271→    "validate_completion",
   272→    "validate_stage",
   273→]
   274→

1→"""Thin wrapper around review runner_process for triage stage execution."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import subprocess
     6→import time
     7→from collections.abc import Callable
     8→from pathlib import Path
     9→
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.review._runner_process_types import CodexBatchRunnerDeps

    12→    codex_batch_command,

    17→
    18→def _output_file_has_text(output_file: Path) -> bool:
    19→    """Return True when the output file exists and contains non-empty text."""
    20→    if not output_file.exists():
    21→        return False
    22→    try:
    23→        return len(output_file.read_text().strip()) > 0
    24→    except OSError:
    25→        return False
    26→
    27→
    28→def run_triage_stage(
    29→    *,
    30→    prompt: str,
    31→    repo_root: Path,
    32→    output_file: Path,
    33→    log_file: Path,
    34→    timeout_seconds: int = 1800,
    35→    validate_output_fn: Callable[[Path], bool] | None = None,
    36→) -> int:
    37→    """Execute a triage stage via codex subprocess. Returns exit code."""
    38→    if validate_output_fn is None:
    39→        validate_output_fn = _output_file_has_text
    40→    deps = CodexBatchRunnerDeps(
    41→        timeout_seconds=timeout_seconds,
    42→        subprocess_run=subprocess.run,
    43→        timeout_error=subprocess.TimeoutExpired,
    44→        safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
    45→        use_popen_runner=True,
    46→        subprocess_popen=subprocess.Popen,
    47→        live_log_interval_seconds=10.0,
    48→        stall_after_output_seconds=120,
    49→        max_retries=1,
    50→        retry_backoff_seconds=5.0,
    51→        sleep_fn=time.sleep,
    52→        validate_output_fn=validate_output_fn,
    53→    )
    54→    return run_codex_batch(
    55→        prompt=prompt,
    56→        repo_root=repo_root,
    57→        output_file=output_file,
    58→        log_file=log_file,
    59→        deps=deps,
    60→    )
    61→
    62→
    63→__all__ = ["run_triage_stage"]
    64→

    40→def _parse_only_stages(raw: str | None) -> list[str]:
    41→    """Parse --only-stages comma-separated string into validated stage list."""
    42→    if not raw:
    43→        return list(_STAGES)
    44→    stages = [s.strip().lower() for s in raw.split(",") if s.strip()]
    45→    for s in stages:

    47→            raise ValueError(f"Unknown stage: {s!r}. Valid: {', '.join(_STAGES)}")

    51→def _ensure_triage_started(

    56→    if not has_triage_in_queue(plan):

    59→        meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})

   116→
   117→def _merge_observe_outputs(
   118→    batch_outputs: list[tuple[list[str], Path]],
   119→) -> str:
   120→    """Concatenate batch outputs with dimension headers into single observe report."""
   121→    parts: list[str] = []
   122→    for dims, output_file in batch_outputs:
   123→        header = f"## Dimensions: {', '.join(dims)}"
   124→        content = ""
   125→        if output_file.exists():
   126→            try:
   127→                content = output_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace").strip()
   128→            except OSError:
   129→                content = "(batch output missing)"
   130→        if not content:
   131→            content = "(batch produced no output)"
   132→        parts.append(f"{header}\n\n{content}")
   133→    return "\n\n---\n\n".join(parts)
   134→
   135→
   136→def _run_observe(
   137→    *,
   138→    si,
   139→    repo_root: Path,
   140→    prompts_dir: Path,
   141→    output_dir: Path,
   142→    logs_dir: Path,
   143→    timeout_seconds: int,
   144→    dry_run: bool = False,
   145→    append_run_log=None,
   146→) -> tuple[bool, str]:
   147→    """Run observe stage via codex subprocess batches.
   148→
   149→    Returns (ok, merged_report) where ok is True on success, False on failure.
   150→    """
   151→    from functools import partial
   152→
   153→    from desloppify.app.commands.review._runner_parallel_types import BatchProgressEvent
   154→
   155→    from .codex_runner import _output_file_has_text, run_triage_stage
   156→
   157→    _log = append_run_log or (lambda _msg: None)
   158→
   159→    batches = group_issues_into_observe_batches(si)
   160→    total = len(batches)
   161→    print(colorize(f"\n  Observe: splitting into {total} parallel batches.", "bold"))
   162→    _log(f"observe-parallel batches={total}")
   163→
   164→    # Build prompts and tasks
   165→    tasks: dict[int, object] = {}
   166→    batch_meta: list[tuple[list[str], Path]] = []
   167→
   168→    for i, (dims, issues_subset) in enumerate(batches):
   169→        prompt = build_observe_batch_prompt(
   170→            batch_index=i + 1,
   171→            total_batches=total,
   172→            dimension_group=dims,
   173→            issues_subset=issues_subset,
   174→            repo_root=repo_root,
   175→        )
   176→        prompt_file = prompts_dir / f"observe_batch_{i}.md"
   177→        safe_write_text(prompt_file, prompt)
   178→
   179→        output_file = output_dir / f"observe_batch_{i}.raw.txt"
   180→        log_file = logs_dir / f"observe_batch_{i}.log"
   181→        batch_meta.append((dims, output_file))
   182→
   183→        if not dry_run:
   184→            tasks[i] = partial(
   185→                run_triage_stage,
   186→                prompt=prompt,
   187→                repo_root=repo_root,
   188→                output_file=output_file,
   189→                log_file=log_file,
   190→                timeout_seconds=timeout_seconds,
   191→                validate_output_fn=_output_file_has_text,
   192→            )
   193→
   194→        dim_list = ", ".join(dims)
   195→        print(colorize(f"    Batch {i + 1}: {len(issues_subset)} issues ({dim_list})", "dim"))
   196→        _log(f"observe-batch batch={i + 1} issues={len(issues_subset)} dims={dim_list}")
   197→
   198→    if dry_run:
   199→        print(colorize("  [dry-run] Would execute parallel observe batches.", "dim"))
   200→        return True, ""
   201→
   202→    # Progress callback — same pattern as review batch execution
   203→    def _progress(event: BatchProgressEvent) -> None:
   204→        idx = event.batch_index
   205→        if event.event == "start":
   206→            print(colorize(f"    Observe batch {idx + 1}/{total} started", "dim"))
   207→            _log(f"observe-batch-start batch={idx + 1}")
   208→        elif event.event == "done":
   209→            elapsed = event.details.get("elapsed_seconds", 0) if event.details else 0
   210→            status = "done" if event.code == 0 else f"failed ({event.code})"
   211→            tone = "dim" if event.code == 0 else "yellow"
   212→            print(colorize(f"    Observe batch {idx + 1}/{total} {status} in {int(elapsed)}s", tone))
   213→            _log(f"observe-batch-done batch={idx + 1} code={event.code} elapsed={int(elapsed)}s")
   214→        elif event.event == "heartbeat":
   215→            details = event.details or {}
   216→            active = details.get("active_batches", [])
   217→            elapsed_map = details.get("elapsed_seconds", {})
   218→            if active:
   219→                parts = [f"#{i + 1}:{int(elapsed_map.get(i, 0))}s" for i in active[:6]]
   220→                print(colorize(f"    Observe heartbeat: {len(active)}/{total} active ({', '.join(parts)})", "dim"))
   221→
   222→    def _error_log(batch_index: int, exc: Exception) -> None:
   223→        _log(f"observe-batch-error batch={batch_index + 1} error={exc}")
   224→
   225→    # Execute in parallel
   226→    failures = execute_batches(
   227→        tasks=tasks,
   228→        options=BatchExecutionOptions(run_parallel=True, heartbeat_seconds=15.0),
   229→        progress_fn=_progress,
   230→        error_log_fn=_error_log,
   231→    )
   232→
   233→    if failures:
   234→        print(colorize(
   235→            f"  Observe: {len(failures)} batch(es) failed: {failures}",
   236→            "red",
   237→        ))
   238→        for idx in failures:
   239→            log_file = logs_dir / f"observe_batch_{idx}.log"
   240→            print(colorize(f"    Check log: {log_file}", "dim"))
   241→        _log(f"observe-parallel-failed failures={failures}")
   242→        return False, ""
   243→
   244→    merged = _merge_observe_outputs(batch_meta)
   245→    print(colorize(f"  Observe: merged {total} batch outputs ({len(merged)} chars).", "green"))

   524→def _run_sense_check(
