```
   443→def _enrich_report_or_error(report: str | None) -> str | None:
   444→    if not report:
   445→        print(colorize("  --report is required for --stage enrich.", "red"))
   446→        print(colorize("  Summarize the enrichment work you did:", "dim"))
   447→        print(colorize("  - Which clusters did you add detail/refs to?", "dim"))
   448→        print(colorize("  - Are steps specific enough for an executor with zero context?", "dim"))
   449→        print(colorize("  - Did you link issue_refs so steps auto-complete on resolve?", "dim"))
   450→        return None
   451→    if len(report) < 100:
   452→        print(colorize(f"  Report too short: {len(report)} chars (minimum 100).", "red"))
   453→        print(colorize("  Explain what enrichment you did and why steps are executor-ready.", "dim"))
   454→        return None
   455→    return report
   456→
   457→
   458→def _require_enrich_stage_for_complete(
   459→    *,
   460→    plan: dict,
   461→    meta: dict,
   462→    stages: dict,
   463→) -> bool:
   464→    if "enrich" in stages:
   465→        return True
   466→    if "organize" not in stages:
   467→        # Fall through to existing organize requirement
   468→        return _require_organize_stage_for_complete(plan=plan, meta=meta, stages=stages)
   469→
   470→    shallow = _shallow_steps(plan)
   471→    if shallow:
   472→        print(colorize("  Cannot complete: enrich stage not done.", "red"))
   473→        print(colorize(f"  {len(shallow)} cluster(s) have steps without detail or issue_refs:", "yellow"))
   474→        for name, bare, total in shallow[:5]:
   475→            print(colorize(f"    {name}: {bare}/{total} steps need enrichment", "yellow"))
   476→        print(colorize(
   477→            '  Fix: desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail "sub-details"',
   478→            "dim",
   479→        ))
   480→        print(colorize('  Then: desloppify plan triage --stage enrich --report "..."', "dim"))
   481→    else:
   482→        print(colorize("  Cannot complete: enrich stage not recorded.", "red"))
   483→        print(colorize("  Steps look enriched. Record the stage:", "dim"))
   484→        print(colorize('    desloppify plan triage --stage enrich --report "..."', "dim"))
   485→    return False
   486→
   487→
   488→def _auto_confirm_enrich_for_complete(
   489→    *,
   490→    plan: dict,
   491→    stages: dict,
   492→    attestation: str | None,
   493→) -> bool:
   494→    if stages.get("enrich", {}).get("confirmed_at"):
   495→        return True
   496→    if "enrich" not in stages:
   497→        return False
   498→    if not attestation or len(attestation.strip()) < _MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN:
   499→        print(colorize("  Cannot complete: enrich stage not confirmed.", "red"))
   500→        print(colorize("  Run: desloppify plan triage --confirm enrich", "dim"))
   501→        print(colorize("  Or pass --attestation to auto-confirm enrich inline.", "dim"))
   502→        return False
   503→
   504→    # Re-validate shallow steps at auto-confirm time
   505→    shallow = _shallow_steps(plan)
   506→    if shallow:
   507→        total_bare = sum(n for _, n, _ in shallow)
   508→        print(colorize(f"  Cannot auto-confirm enrich: {total_bare} step(s) still lack detail or issue_refs.", "red"))
   509→        for name, bare, total in shallow[:5]:
   510→            print(colorize(f"    {name}: {bare}/{total} steps", "yellow"))
   511→        print(colorize('  Fix: desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail "sub-details"', "dim"))
   512→        return False
   513→
   514→    cluster_names = [
   515→        name for name in plan.get("clusters", {}) if not plan["clusters"][name].get("auto")
   516→    ]
   517→    validation_err = _validate_attestation(
   518→        attestation.strip(),
   519→        "enrich",
   520→        cluster_names=cluster_names,
   521→    )
   522→    if validation_err:
   523→        print(colorize(f"  {validation_err}", "red"))
   524→        return False
   525→    stages["enrich"]["confirmed_at"] = utc_now()
   526→    stages["enrich"]["confirmed_text"] = attestation.strip()
   527→    save_plan(plan)
   528→    print(colorize("  ✓ Enrich auto-confirmed via --attestation.", "green"))
   529→    return True
   530→
   531→
   532→def _require_sense_check_stage_for_complete(

   538→    if "sense-check" in stages:

   540→    if "enrich" not in stages:

   542→
   543→    print(colorize("  Cannot complete: sense-check stage not recorded.", "red"))
   544→    print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage sense-check --report "..."', "dim"))
   545→    return False

   658→def _completion_clusters_valid(plan: dict, state: dict | None = None) -> bool:
   659→    manual_clusters = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   660→    if not manual_clusters:
   661→        any_clusters = [
   662→            name
   663→            for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
   664→            if cluster.get("issue_ids")
   665→        ]
   666→        if not any_clusters:
   667→            print(colorize("  Cannot complete: no clusters with issues exist.", "red"))
   668→            print(colorize('  Create clusters: desloppify plan cluster create <name> --description "..."', "dim"))
   669→            return False
   670→
   671→    gaps = _unenriched_clusters(plan)
   672→    if gaps:
   673→        print(colorize(f"  Cannot complete: {len(gaps)} cluster(s) still need enrichment.", "red"))
   674→        for name, missing in gaps:
   675→            print(colorize(f"    {name}: missing {', '.join(missing)}", "yellow"))
   676→        print(
   677→            colorize(
   678→                "  Small clusters (<5 issues) need at least 1 action step per issue.",
   679→                "dim",
   680→            )
   681→        )
   682→        print(
   683→            colorize(
   684→                '  Fix: desloppify plan cluster update <name> --description "..." --steps "step1" "step2"',
   685→                "dim",
   686→            )
   687→        )
   688→        return False
   689→
   690→    # Check for unclustered review issues
   691→    unclustered = _unclustered_review_issues(plan, state)
   692→    if unclustered:
   693→        print(colorize(f"  Cannot complete: {len(unclustered)} review issue(s) have no action plan.", "red"))
   694→        for fid in unclustered[:5]:
   695→            short = fid.rsplit("::", 2)[-2] if "::" in fid else fid
   696→            print(colorize(f"    {short}", "yellow"))
   697→        if len(unclustered) > 5:
   698→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(unclustered) - 5} more", "yellow"))
   699→        print(colorize("  Add to a cluster or wontfix each unclustered issue.", "dim"))
   700→        return False
   701→
   702→    return True
   703→

   705→def _resolve_completion_strategy(
   706→    strategy: str | None,
   707→    *,
   708→    meta: dict,
   709→) -> str | None:
   710→    if strategy:
   711→        return strategy
   712→    print(colorize("  --strategy is required.", "red"))
   713→    existing = meta.get("strategy_summary", "")
   714→    if existing:
   715→        print(colorize(f"  Current strategy: {existing}", "dim"))
   716→        print(colorize('  Use --strategy "same" to keep it, or provide a new summary.', "dim"))
   717→    else:
   718→        print(
   719→            colorize(
   720→                '  Provide --strategy "execution plan describing priorities, ordering, and verification approach"',
   721→                "dim",
   722→            )
   723→        )
   724→    return None
   725→
   726→
   727→def _completion_strategy_valid(strategy: str) -> bool:
   728→    if strategy.strip().lower() == "same":
   729→        return True
   730→    if len(strategy.strip()) >= 200:
   731→        return True
   732→    print(colorize(f"  Strategy too short: {len(strategy.strip())} chars (minimum 200).", "red"))
   733→    print(colorize("  The strategy should describe:", "dim"))
   734→    print(colorize("    - Execution order and priorities", "dim"))
   735→    print(colorize("    - What each cluster accomplishes", "dim"))
   736→    print(colorize("    - How to verify the work is correct", "dim"))
   737→    return False
   738→
   739→

   741→    if meta.get("strategy_summary", ""):
   742→        return True
   743→    print(colorize("  Cannot confirm existing: no prior triage has been completed.", "red"))
   744→    print(colorize("  The full OBSERVE → REFLECT → ORGANIZE → COMMIT flow is required the first time.", "dim"))
   745→    print(colorize(f"  Create and enrich clusters, then: {TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE}", "dim"))
   746→    return False

   749→def _confirm_existing_stages_valid(
   750→    *,

   753→    si,
   754→) -> bool:
   755→    if has_only_additions:
   756→        _print_new_issues_since_last(si)
   757→        return True
   758→    if "observe" not in stages:
   759→        print(colorize("  Cannot confirm existing: observe stage not complete.", "red"))
   760→        print(colorize("  You must read issues first.", "dim"))
   761→        print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report "..."', "dim"))
   762→        return False
   763→    if "reflect" not in stages:
   764→        print(colorize("  Cannot confirm existing: reflect stage not complete.", "red"))
   765→        print(colorize("  You must compare against completed work first.", "dim"))
   766→        print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage reflect --report "..."', "dim"))
   767→        return False
   768→    return True

   770→
   771→def _confirm_note_valid(note: str | None) -> bool:
   772→    if not note:
   773→        print(colorize("  --note is required for confirm-existing.", "red"))
   774→        print(colorize('  Explain why the existing plan is still valid (min 100 chars).', "dim"))
   775→        return False
   776→    if len(note) < 100:
   777→        print(colorize(f"  Note too short: {len(note)} chars (minimum 100).", "red"))
   778→        return False
   779→    return True

   781→
   782→def _resolve_confirm_existing_strategy(
   783→    strategy: str | None,
   784→    *,

   787→) -> str | None:
   788→    if strategy:
   789→        return strategy
   790→    if has_only_additions:
   791→        return "same"
   792→    print(colorize("  --strategy is required.", "red"))
   793→    existing = meta.get("strategy_summary", "")
   794→    if existing:
   795→        print(colorize('  Use --strategy "same" to keep it, or provide a new summary.', "dim"))
   796→    return None
   797→
   798→
   799→def _confirm_strategy_valid(strategy: str) -> bool:
   800→    if strategy.strip().lower() == "same":
   801→        return True
   802→    if len(strategy.strip()) >= 200:
   803→        return True
   804→    print(colorize(f"  Strategy too short: {len(strategy.strip())} chars (minimum 200).", "red"))
   805→    return False

   807→
   808→def _confirmed_text_or_error(
   809→    *,
   810→    plan: dict,
   811→    state: dict,
   812→    confirmed: str | None,
   813→) -> str | None:
   814→    if confirmed and len(confirmed.strip()) >= _MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN:
   815→        return confirmed.strip()
   816→    print(colorize("  Current plan:", "bold"))
   817→    show_plan_summary(plan, state)
   818→    if confirmed:
   819→        print(
   820→            colorize(
   821→                f"\n  --confirmed text too short ({len(confirmed.strip())} chars, min {_MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN}).",
   822→                "red",
   823→            )
   824→        )
   825→    print(colorize('\n  Add --confirmed "I validate this plan..." to proceed.', "dim"))
   826→    return None
   827→
   828→
   829→def _note_cites_new_issues_or_error(note: str, si) -> bool:
   830→    new_ids = si.new_since_last
   831→    if not new_ids:
   832→        return True
   833→    valid_ids = set(si.open_issues.keys())
   834→    cited = extract_issue_citations(note, valid_ids)
   835→    new_cited = cited & new_ids
   836→    if new_cited:
   837→        return True
   838→    print(colorize("  Note must cite at least 1 new/changed issue.", "red"))
   839→    print(colorize(f"  {len(new_ids)} new issue(s) since last triage:", "dim"))
   840→    for fid in sorted(new_ids)[:5]:
   841→        print(colorize(f"    {fid}", "dim"))
   842→    if len(new_ids) > 5:
   843→        print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(new_ids) - 5} more", "dim"))
   844→    return False
   845→
```

```
1→"""Post-subagent validation and auto-attestation for triage runners."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from pathlib import Path

    23→def validate_stage(
    24→    stage: str,
    25→    plan: dict,
    26→    state: dict,
    27→    repo_root: Path,
    28→    *,
    29→    triage_input: TriageInput | None = None,
    30→) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    31→    """Check subagent completed stage correctly. Returns (ok, error_msg)."""
    32→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    33→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
    34→
    35→    if stage == "observe":
    36→        if "observe" not in stages:
    37→            return False, "Observe stage not recorded."
    38→        report = stages["observe"].get("report", "")
    39→        if len(report) < 100:
    40→            return False, f"Observe report too short ({len(report)} chars, need 100+)."
    41→        # Check citation ratio — must reference specific issues
    42→        cited = stages["observe"].get("cited_ids", [])
    43→        issue_count = stages["observe"].get("issue_count", 0)
    44→        if issue_count > 0:
    45→            min_citations = min(5, max(1, issue_count // 10))
    46→            if len(cited) < min_citations:
    47→                return False, (
    48→                    f"Observe report cites only {len(cited)} issue(s) "
    49→                    f"(need {min_citations}+). Reference specific issue "
    50→                    f"hashes to prove you read them."
    51→                )
    52→        return True, ""
    53→
    54→    if stage == "reflect":
    55→        if "reflect" not in stages:
    56→            return False, "Reflect stage not recorded."
    57→        report = stages["reflect"].get("report", "")
    58→        if len(report) < 100:
    59→            return False, f"Reflect report too short ({len(report)} chars, need 100+)."
    60→        return True, ""
    61→
    62→    if stage == "organize":
    63→        if "organize" not in stages:
    64→            return False, "Organize stage not recorded."
    65→        manual = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
    66→        if not manual:
    67→            return False, "No manual clusters with issues exist."
    68→        gaps = _unenriched_clusters(plan)
    69→        if gaps:
    70→            names = ", ".join(n for n, _ in gaps)
    71→            return False, f"Unenriched clusters: {names}"
    72→        unclustered = _unclustered_review_issues(plan, state)
    73→        if unclustered:
    74→            return False, f"{len(unclustered)} review issue(s) not in any cluster."
    75→        # Advisory warnings (non-blocking but informational)
    76→        warnings: list[str] = []
    77→        overlaps = _cluster_file_overlaps(plan)
    78→        if overlaps:
    79→            warnings.append(f"{len(overlaps)} cluster pair(s) share files without dependencies")
    80→        scattered = _clusters_with_directory_scatter(plan)
    81→        if scattered:
    82→            names = ", ".join(n for n, _, _ in scattered)
    83→            warnings.append(f"Theme-grouped clusters (5+ dirs): {names}")
    84→        high_ratio = _clusters_with_high_step_ratio(plan)
    85→        if high_ratio:
    86→            names = ", ".join(n for n, _, _, _ in high_ratio)
    87→            warnings.append(f"1:1 step-to-issue ratio: {names}")
    88→        # Orphaned clusters (steps but no issues)
    89→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    90→        orphaned = [
    91→            n for n, c in clusters.items()
    92→            if not c.get("auto") and not c.get("issue_ids") and c.get("action_steps")
    93→        ]
    94→        if orphaned:
    95→            warnings.append(f"Orphaned clusters (steps, no issues): {', '.join(orphaned)}")
    96→        if warnings:
    97→            # Return success with advisory message
    98→            return True, "Advisory: " + "; ".join(warnings)
    99→        return True, ""
   100→
   101→    if stage == "enrich":
   102→        if "enrich" not in stages:
   103→            return False, "Enrich stage not recorded."
   104→        shallow = _shallow_steps(plan)
   105→        if shallow:
   106→            total = sum(n for _, n, _ in shallow)
   107→            return False, f"{total} step(s) still lack detail or issue_refs."
   108→        bad_paths = _steps_with_bad_paths(plan, repo_root)
   109→        if bad_paths:
   110→            total = sum(len(bp) for _, _, bp in bad_paths)
   111→            return False, f"{total} file path(s) in step details don't exist on disk."
   112→        # Effort tags are now blocking
   113→        untagged = _steps_without_effort(plan)
   114→        if untagged:
   115→            total = sum(n for _, n, _ in untagged)
   116→            return False, f"{total} step(s) have no effort tag (trivial/small/medium/large)."
   117→        # Issue refs are now blocking
   118→        no_refs = _steps_missing_issue_refs(plan)
   119→        if no_refs:
   120→            total = sum(n for _, n, _ in no_refs)
   121→            return False, f"{total} step(s) have no issue_refs for traceability."
   122→        # Vague detail is now blocking
   123→        vague = _steps_with_vague_detail(plan, repo_root)
   124→        if vague:
   125→            return False, (
   126→                f"{len(vague)} step(s) have vague detail (< 80 chars, no file paths). "
   127→                f"Executor-ready means: file path + specific instruction."
   128→            )
   129→        return True, ""
   130→
   131→    if stage == "sense-check":
   132→        if "sense-check" not in stages:
   133→            return False, "Sense-check stage not recorded."
   134→        report = stages["sense-check"].get("report", "")
   135→        if len(report) < 100:
   136→            return False, f"Sense-check report too short ({len(report)} chars, need 100+)."
   137→        # Re-run all enrich-level checks (subagents may have introduced issues)
   138→        shallow = _shallow_steps(plan)
   139→        if shallow:
   140→            total = sum(n for _, n, _ in shallow)
   141→            return False, f"{total} step(s) still lack detail or issue_refs after sense-check."
   142→        bad_paths = _steps_with_bad_paths(plan, repo_root)
   143→        if bad_paths:
   144→            total = sum(len(bp) for _, _, bp in bad_paths)
   145→            return False, f"{total} file path(s) don't exist on disk after sense-check."
   146→        untagged = _steps_without_effort(plan)
   147→        if untagged:
   148→            total = sum(n for _, n, _ in untagged)
   149→            return False, f"{total} step(s) have no effort tag after sense-check."
   150→        no_refs = _steps_missing_issue_refs(plan)
   151→        if no_refs:
   152→            total = sum(n for _, n, _ in no_refs)
   153→            return False, f"{total} step(s) have no issue_refs after sense-check."
   154→        vague = _steps_with_vague_detail(plan, repo_root)
   155→        if vague:
   156→            return False, f"{len(vague)} step(s) have vague detail after sense-check."
   157→        return True, ""
   158→
   159→    return False, f"Unknown stage: {stage}"
   160→
   161→
   162→def validate_completion(
   163→    plan: dict,
   164→    state: dict,
   165→    repo_root: Path,
   166→) -> tuple[bool, str]:
   167→    """Validate plan is ready for triage completion. Returns (ok, error_msg)."""
   168→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   169→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   170→
   171→    for required in ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "sense-check"):
   172→        if required not in stages:
   173→            return False, f"Stage {required} not recorded."
   174→        if not stages[required].get("confirmed_at"):
   175→            return False, f"Stage {required} not confirmed."
   176→
   177→    manual = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   178→    if not manual:
   179→        return False, "No manual clusters with issues."
   180→
   181→    gaps = _unenriched_clusters(plan)
   182→    if gaps:
   183→        return False, f"{len(gaps)} cluster(s) still need enrichment."
   184→
   185→    unclustered = _unclustered_review_issues(plan, state)
   186→    if unclustered:
   187→        return False, f"{len(unclustered)} review issue(s) not in any cluster."
   188→
   189→    # Quality warnings (non-blocking but logged)
   190→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   191→    # Check dependency ordering
   192→    for name, c in clusters.items():
   193→        deps = c.get("depends_on_clusters", [])
   194→        if name in deps:
   195→            return False, f"Cluster {name} depends on itself."
   196→
   197→    # Check for all-trivial clusters
   198→    all_trivial_clusters = []
   199→    total_steps = 0
   200→    for name, c in clusters.items():
   201→        if c.get("auto") or not c.get("issue_ids"):
   202→            continue
   203→        steps = c.get("action_steps") or []
   204→        total_steps += len(steps)
   205→        if steps and all(
   206→            isinstance(s, dict) and s.get("effort") == "trivial" for s in steps
   207→        ):
   208→            all_trivial_clusters.append(name)
   209→
   210→    return True, ""
   211→
   212→
   213→def build_auto_attestation(
   214→    stage: str,
   215→    plan: dict,
   216→    triage_input: TriageInput,
   217→) -> str:
   218→    """Generate valid 80+ char attestation referencing real dimensions/cluster names."""
   219→    if stage == "observe":
   220→        _by_dim, dim_names = observe_dimension_breakdown(triage_input)
   221→        top_dims = dim_names[:3]
   222→        dims_str = ", ".join(top_dims)
   223→        return (
   224→            f"I have thoroughly analysed {len(triage_input.open_issues)} issues "
   225→            f"across dimensions including {dims_str}, identifying themes, "
   226→            f"root causes, and contradictions across the codebase."
   227→        )
   228→
   229→    if stage == "reflect":
   230→        _by_dim, dim_names = observe_dimension_breakdown(triage_input)
   231→        top_dims = dim_names[:3]
   232→        dims_str = ", ".join(top_dims)
   233→        return (
   234→            f"My strategy accounts for {len(triage_input.open_issues)} issues "
   235→            f"across dimensions including {dims_str}, comparing against "
   236→            f"resolved history and forming priorities for execution."
   237→        )
   238→
   239→    if stage == "organize":
   240→        cluster_names = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   241→        names_str = ", ".join(cluster_names[:3])
   242→        return (
   243→            f"I have organized all review issues into clusters including "
   244→            f"{names_str}, with descriptions, action steps, and clear "
   245→            f"priority ordering based on root cause analysis."
   246→        )
   247→
   248→    if stage == "enrich":
   249→        cluster_names = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   250→        names_str = ", ".join(cluster_names[:3])
   251→        return (
   252→            f"Steps in clusters including {names_str} are executor-ready with "
   253→            f"detail, file paths, issue refs, and effort tags, verified "
   254→            f"against the actual codebase."
   255→        )
   256→
   257→    if stage == "sense-check":
   258→        cluster_names = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   259→        names_str = ", ".join(cluster_names[:3])
   260→        return (
   261→            f"Content and structure verified for clusters including {names_str}. "
   262→            f"All step details are factually accurate, cross-cluster dependencies "
   263→            f"are safe, and enrich-level checks pass."
   264→        )
   265→
   266→    return f"Stage {stage} completed with thorough analysis of all available data and verified against codebase."
   267→
   268→
   269→__all__ = [
   270→    "build_auto_attestation",
   271→    "validate_completion",
   272→    "validate_stage",
   273→]
   274→
```
```

    40→def _parse_only_stages(raw: str | None) -> list[str]:
    41→    """Parse --only-stages comma-separated string into validated stage list."""
    42→    if not raw:
    43→        return list(_STAGES)
    44→    stages = [s.strip().lower() for s in raw.split(",") if s.strip()]
    45→    for s in stages:
    46→        if s not in _STAGES:
    47→            raise ValueError(f"Unknown stage: {s!r}. Valid: {', '.join(_STAGES)}")
    48→    return stages

    51→def _ensure_triage_started(
    52→    plan: dict,
    53→    services: TriageServices,
    54→) -> dict:
    55→    """Auto-start triage if not started. Returns updated plan."""
    56→    if not has_triage_in_queue(plan):

    58→        meta = plan.setdefault("epic_triage_meta", {})
    59→        meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
    60→        services.save_plan(plan)
    61→        print(colorize("  Planning mode auto-started.", "cyan"))

   113→    print("    - Sense-check launches TWO parallel subagents (content + structure).")
   114→    print("    - If a stage fails validation, fix and re-record.")

   116→
   117→def _merge_observe_outputs(
   118→    batch_outputs: list[tuple[list[str], Path]],
   119→) -> str:
   120→    """Concatenate batch outputs with dimension headers into single observe report."""
   121→    parts: list[str] = []
   122→    for dims, output_file in batch_outputs:
   123→        header = f"## Dimensions: {', '.join(dims)}"
   124→        content = ""
   125→        if output_file.exists():
   126→            try:
   127→                content = output_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace").strip()
   128→            except OSError:
   129→                content = "(batch output missing)"
   130→        if not content:
   131→            content = "(batch produced no output)"
   132→        parts.append(f"{header}\n\n{content}")
   133→    return "\n\n---\n\n".join(parts)
   134→
   135→
   136→def _run_observe(
   137→    *,
   138→    si,
   139→    repo_root: Path,
   140→    prompts_dir: Path,
   141→    output_dir: Path,
   142→    logs_dir: Path,
   143→    timeout_seconds: int,
   144→    dry_run: bool = False,
   145→    append_run_log=None,
   146→) -> tuple[bool, str]:
   147→    """Run observe stage via codex subprocess batches.
   148→
   149→    Returns (ok, merged_report) where ok is True on success, False on failure.
   150→    """
   151→    from functools import partial
   152→
   153→    from desloppify.app.commands.review._runner_parallel_types import BatchProgressEvent
   154→
   155→    from .codex_runner import _output_file_has_text, run_triage_stage
   156→
   157→    _log = append_run_log or (lambda _msg: None)
   158→
   159→    batches = group_issues_into_observe_batches(si)
   160→    total = len(batches)
   161→    print(colorize(f"\n  Observe: splitting into {total} parallel batches.", "bold"))
   162→    _log(f"observe-parallel batches={total}")
   163→
   164→    # Build prompts and tasks
   165→    tasks: dict[int, object] = {}
   166→    batch_meta: list[tuple[list[str], Path]] = []
   167→
   168→    for i, (dims, issues_subset) in enumerate(batches):
   169→        prompt = build_observe_batch_prompt(
   170→            batch_index=i + 1,
   171→            total_batches=total,
   172→            dimension_group=dims,
   173→            issues_subset=issues_subset,
   174→            repo_root=repo_root,
   175→        )
   176→        prompt_file = prompts_dir / f"observe_batch_{i}.md"
   177→        safe_write_text(prompt_file, prompt)
   178→
   179→        output_file = output_dir / f"observe_batch_{i}.raw.txt"
   180→        log_file = logs_dir / f"observe_batch_{i}.log"
   181→        batch_meta.append((dims, output_file))
   182→
   183→        if not dry_run:
   184→            tasks[i] = partial(
   185→                run_triage_stage,
   186→                prompt=prompt,
   187→                repo_root=repo_root,
   188→                output_file=output_file,
   189→                log_file=log_file,
   190→                timeout_seconds=timeout_seconds,
   191→                validate_output_fn=_output_file_has_text,
   192→            )
   193→
   194→        dim_list = ", ".join(dims)
   195→        print(colorize(f"    Batch {i + 1}: {len(issues_subset)} issues ({dim_list})", "dim"))
   196→        _log(f"observe-batch batch={i + 1} issues={len(issues_subset)} dims={dim_list}")
   197→
   198→    if dry_run:
   199→        print(colorize("  [dry-run] Would execute parallel observe batches.", "dim"))
   200→        return True, ""
   201→
   202→    # Progress callback — same pattern as review batch execution
   203→    def _progress(event: BatchProgressEvent) -> None:
   204→        idx = event.batch_index
   205→        if event.event == "start":
   206→            print(colorize(f"    Observe batch {idx + 1}/{total} started", "dim"))
   207→            _log(f"observe-batch-start batch={idx + 1}")
   208→        elif event.event == "done":
   209→            elapsed = event.details.get("elapsed_seconds", 0) if event.details else 0
   210→            status = "done" if event.code == 0 else f"failed ({event.code})"
   211→            tone = "dim" if event.code == 0 else "yellow"
   212→            print(colorize(f"    Observe batch {idx + 1}/{total} {status} in {int(elapsed)}s", tone))
   213→            _log(f"observe-batch-done batch={idx + 1} code={event.code} elapsed={int(elapsed)}s")
   214→        elif event.event == "heartbeat":
   215→            details = event.details or {}
   216→            active = details.get("active_batches", [])
   217→            elapsed_map = details.get("elapsed_seconds", {})
   218→            if active:
   219→                parts = [f"#{i + 1}:{int(elapsed_map.get(i, 0))}s" for i in active[:6]]
   220→                print(colorize(f"    Observe heartbeat: {len(active)}/{total} active ({', '.join(parts)})", "dim"))
   221→
   222→    def _error_log(batch_index: int, exc: Exception) -> None:
   223→        _log(f"observe-batch-error batch={batch_index + 1} error={exc}")
   224→
   225→    # Execute in parallel
   226→    failures = execute_batches(
   227→        tasks=tasks,
   228→        options=BatchExecutionOptions(run_parallel=True, heartbeat_seconds=15.0),
   229→        progress_fn=_progress,
   230→        error_log_fn=_error_log,
   231→    )
   232→
   233→    if failures:
   234→        print(colorize(
   235→            f"  Observe: {len(failures)} batch(es) failed: {failures}",
   236→            "red",
   237→        ))
   238→        for idx in failures:
   239→            log_file = logs_dir / f"observe_batch_{idx}.log"
   240→            print(colorize(f"    Check log: {log_file}", "dim"))
   241→        _log(f"observe-parallel-failed failures={failures}")
   242→        return False, ""
   243→
   244→    merged = _merge_observe_outputs(batch_meta)
   245→    print(colorize(f"  Observe: merged {total} batch outputs ({len(merged)} chars).", "green"))
   246→    _log(f"observe-parallel-done merged_chars={len(merged)}")
   247→    return True, merged
   248→
   249→
   250→def _run_codex_pipeline(
   251→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   252→    *,
   253→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   254→) -> None:
   255→    """Run triage stages via Codex subprocesses (automated pipeline)."""
   256→    from .codex_runner import run_triage_stage
   257→
   258→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   259→    timeout_seconds = int(getattr(args, "stage_timeout_seconds", 1800) or 1800)
   260→    dry_run = bool(getattr(args, "dry_run", False))
   261→
   262→    try:
   263→        stages_to_run = _parse_only_stages(getattr(args, "only_stages", None))
   264→    except ValueError as exc:
   265→        print(colorize(f"  {exc}", "red"))
   266→        return
   267→
   268→    repo_root = get_project_root()
   269→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   270→    _ensure_triage_started(plan, resolved_services)
   271→
   272→    # Create run directory
   273→    stamp = _run_stamp()
   274→    desloppify_dir = repo_root / ".desloppify"
   275→    run_dir = desloppify_dir / "triage_runs" / stamp
   276→    prompts_dir = run_dir / "prompts"
   277→    output_dir = run_dir / "output"
   278→    logs_dir = run_dir / "logs"
   279→    for d in (prompts_dir, output_dir, logs_dir):
   280→        d.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   281→
   282→    # Run log — same pattern as review batch execution
   283→    run_log_path = run_dir / "run.log"
   284→    append_run_log = make_run_log_writer(run_log_path)
   285→    append_run_log(
   286→        f"run-start runner=codex stages={','.join(stages_to_run)} "
   287→        f"timeout={timeout_seconds}s dry_run={dry_run}"
   288→    )
   289→
   290→    print(colorize(f"  Run artifacts: {run_dir}", "dim"))
   291→    print(colorize(f"  Live run log:  {run_log_path}", "dim"))
   292→
   293→    # Collect triage input
   294→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
   295→    state = runtime.state
   296→
   297→    prior_reports: dict[str, str] = {}
   298→    stage_results: dict[str, dict] = {}
   299→    pipeline_start = time.monotonic()
   300→
   301→    for stage in stages_to_run:
   302→        plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   303→        meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   304→        stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   305→
   306→        # Skip if already confirmed (resume support)
   307→        if stage in stages and stages[stage].get("confirmed_at"):
   308→            print(colorize(f"  Stage {stage}: already confirmed, skipping.", "green"))
   309→            append_run_log(f"stage-skip stage={stage} reason=already_confirmed")
   310→            stage_results[stage] = {"status": "skipped"}
   311→            report = stages[stage].get("report", "")
   312→            if report:
   313→                prior_reports[stage] = report
   314→            continue
   315→
   316→        stage_start = time.monotonic()
   317→        append_run_log(f"stage-start stage={stage}")
   318→
   319→        # Collect fresh triage input each stage
   320→        si = resolved_services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
   321→
   322→        # Parallel observe path
   323→        used_parallel = False
   324→        if stage == "observe":
   325→            parallel_ok, merged_report = _run_observe(
   326→                si=si,
   327→                repo_root=repo_root,
   328→                prompts_dir=prompts_dir,
   329→                output_dir=output_dir,
   330→                logs_dir=logs_dir,
   331→                timeout_seconds=timeout_seconds,
   332→                dry_run=dry_run,
   333→                append_run_log=append_run_log,
   334→            )
   335→            if parallel_ok is True and dry_run:
   336→                stage_results[stage] = {"status": "dry_run"}
   337→                continue
   338→            if parallel_ok is True and merged_report:
   339→                # Record observe via the stage command with merged report
   340→                from ..stage_flow_commands import cmd_stage_observe
   341→                record_args = argparse.Namespace(
   342→                    stage="observe",
   343→                    report=merged_report,
   344→                    state=getattr(args, "state", None),
   345→                )
   346→                cmd_stage_observe(record_args, services=resolved_services)
   347→                used_parallel = True
   348→            elif parallel_ok is False:
   349→                # Parallel execution failed — abort pipeline
   350→                elapsed = int(time.monotonic() - stage_start)
   351→                print(colorize("  Observe: parallel execution failed. Aborting.", "red"))
   352→                append_run_log(f"stage-failed stage=observe elapsed={elapsed}s reason=parallel_execution_failed")
   353→                stage_results[stage] = {"status": "failed", "elapsed_seconds": elapsed}
   354→                _write_triage_run_summary(run_dir, stamp, stages_to_run, stage_results, append_run_log)
   355→                return
   356→            # parallel_ok is None → not applicable, fall through to single path
   357→
   358→        # Parallel sense-check path
   359→        if stage == "sense-check":
   360→            parallel_ok, merged_report = _run_sense_check(
   361→                plan=plan,
   362→                repo_root=repo_root,
   363→                prompts_dir=prompts_dir,
   364→                output_dir=output_dir,
   365→                logs_dir=logs_dir,
   366→                timeout_seconds=timeout_seconds,
   367→                dry_run=dry_run,
   368→                append_run_log=append_run_log,
   369→            )
   370→            if parallel_ok is True and dry_run:
   371→                stage_results[stage] = {"status": "dry_run"}
   372→                continue
   373→            if parallel_ok is True and merged_report:
   374→                # Subagents mutated plan via CLI; reload and record stage
   375→                plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   376→                from ..stage_flow_commands import cmd_stage_sense_check
   377→                record_args = argparse.Namespace(
   378→                    stage="sense-check",
   379→                    report=merged_report,
   380→                    state=getattr(args, "state", None),
   381→                )
   382→                cmd_stage_sense_check(record_args, services=resolved_services)
   383→                used_parallel = True
   384→            elif parallel_ok is False:
   385→                elapsed = int(time.monotonic() - stage_start)
   386→                print(colorize("  Sense-check: parallel execution failed. Aborting.", "red"))
   387→                append_run_log(f"stage-failed stage=sense-check elapsed={elapsed}s reason=parallel_execution_failed")
   388→                stage_results[stage] = {"status": "failed", "elapsed_seconds": elapsed}
   389→                _write_triage_run_summary(run_dir, stamp, stages_to_run, stage_results, append_run_log)
   390→                return
   391→
   392→        # Single-subprocess path (non-observe/sense-check stages, or observe with too few issues)
   393→        if not used_parallel:
   394→            prompt = build_stage_prompt(
   395→                stage, si, prior_reports, repo_root=repo_root,
   396→            )
   397→
   398→            prompt_file = prompts_dir / f"{stage}.md"
   399→            safe_write_text(prompt_file, prompt)
   400→
```

```
    47→def _cmd_stage_observe(
    48→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    49→    *,
    50→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
    51→) -> None:
    52→    """Record the OBSERVE stage: agent analyses themes and root causes.
    53→
    54→    No citation gate — the point is genuine analysis, not ID-stuffing.
    55→    Just requires a 100-char report describing what the agent observed.
    56→    """
    57→    report: str | None = getattr(args, "report", None)
    58→
    59→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
    60→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
    61→    state = runtime.state
    62→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
    63→
    64→    # Auto-start: inject triage stage IDs if not present
    65→    # NOTE: preserve existing stage data — don't clear triage_stages.
    66→    # Only _cmd_triage_start (explicit --start) should clear stages.
    67→    if not has_triage_in_queue(plan):
    68→        inject_triage_stages(plan)
    69→        meta = plan.setdefault("epic_triage_meta", {})
    70→        meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
    71→        resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
    72→        print(colorize("  Planning mode auto-started (6 stages queued).", "cyan"))
    73→
    74→    meta = plan.setdefault("epic_triage_meta", {})
    75→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
    76→    existing_stage = stages.get("observe")
    77→
    78→    # Jump-back: reuse existing report if no --report provided
    79→    report, is_reuse = _resolve_reusable_report(report, existing_stage)
    80→    if not report:
    81→        _print_observe_report_requirement()
    82→        return
    83→
    84→    si = resolved_services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
    85→    issue_count = len(si.open_issues)
    86→
    87→    # Edge case: 0 issues
    88→    if issue_count == 0:
    89→        cleared = _record_observe_stage(
    90→            stages,
    91→            report=report,
    92→            issue_count=0,
    93→            cited_ids=[],
    94→            existing_stage=existing_stage,
    95→            is_reuse=is_reuse,
    96→        )
    97→        resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
    98→        print(colorize("  Observe stage recorded (no issues to analyse).", "green"))
    99→        if is_reuse:
   100→            print(colorize("  Observe data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
   101→            if cleared:
   102→                print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared, stages)
   103→        return
   104→
   105→    # Validation: report length (no citation counting)
   106→    min_chars = 50 if issue_count <= 3 else 100
   107→    if len(report) < min_chars:
   108→        print(colorize(f"  Report too short: {len(report)} chars (minimum {min_chars}).", "red"))
   109→        print(colorize("  Describe themes, root causes, contradictions, and how issues relate.", "dim"))
   110→        return
   111→
   112→    # Save stage (still extract citations for analytics, but don't gate on them)
   113→    valid_ids = set(si.open_issues.keys())
   114→    cited = resolved_services.extract_issue_citations(report, valid_ids)
   115→
   116→    cleared = _record_observe_stage(
   117→        stages,
   118→        report=report,
   119→        issue_count=issue_count,
   120→        cited_ids=sorted(cited),
   121→        existing_stage=existing_stage,
   122→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   123→    )
   124→
   125→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   126→
   127→    resolved_services.append_log_entry(
   128→        plan,
   129→        "triage_observe",
   130→        actor="user",
   131→        detail={"issue_count": issue_count, "cited_ids": sorted(cited), "reuse": is_reuse},
   132→    )
   133→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   134→
   135→    print(
   136→        colorize(
   137→            f"  Observe stage recorded: {issue_count} issues analysed.",
   138→            "green",
   139→        )
   140→    )
   141→    if is_reuse:
   142→        print(colorize("  Observe data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
   143→        if cleared:
   144→            print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared, stages)
   145→    else:
   146→        print(colorize("  Now confirm your analysis.", "yellow"))
   147→        print(colorize("    desloppify plan triage --confirm observe", "dim"))
   148→
   149→    if not is_reuse:
   150→        print_user_message(
   151→            "Observe recorded. Before confirming — did the subagent"
   152→            " verify every issue with code reads? Check: are there"
   153→            " specific file/line citations in the report, or just"
   154→            " restated issue titles? Each issue needs a verdict:"

   156→            " until the analysis is backed by actual code evidence."

   160→def _cmd_stage_reflect(
   161→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   162→    *,
   163→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   164→) -> None:
   165→    """Record the REFLECT stage: compare current issues against completed work.
   166→
   167→    Forces the agent to consider what was previously resolved and whether
   168→    similar issues are recurring. Requires a 100-char report (50 if ≤3 issues).
   169→    If recurring patterns are detected, the report must mention at least one
   170→    recurring dimension name.
   171→    """
   172→    report: str | None = getattr(args, "report", None)
   173→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
   174→
   175→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   176→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
   177→    state = runtime.state
   178→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   179→
   180→    if not has_triage_in_queue(plan):
   181→        print(colorize("  No planning stages in the queue — nothing to reflect on.", "yellow"))
   182→        return
   183→
   184→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   185→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   186→
   187→    # Jump-back: reuse existing report if no --report provided
   188→    existing_stage = stages.get("reflect")
   189→    is_reuse = False
   190→    if not report and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("report"):
   191→        report = existing_stage["report"]
   192→        is_reuse = True
   193→    elif not report:
   194→        _print_reflect_report_requirement()
   195→        return
   196→
   197→    if "observe" not in stages:
   198→        print(colorize("  Cannot reflect: observe stage not complete.", "red"))
   199→        print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report "..."', "dim"))
   200→        return
   201→
   202→    si = resolved_services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
   203→
   204→    # Fold-confirm: auto-confirm observe if attestation provided
   205→    if not _auto_confirm_observe_if_attested(
   206→        plan=plan,
   207→        stages=stages,
   208→        attestation=attestation,
   209→        triage_input=si,
   210→    ):
   211→        return
   212→
   213→    issue_count = len(si.open_issues)
   214→
   215→    # Validation: report length
   216→    min_chars = 50 if issue_count <= 3 else 100
   217→    if len(report) < min_chars:
   218→        print(colorize(f"  Report too short: {len(report)} chars (minimum {min_chars}).", "red"))
   219→        print(colorize("  Describe how current issues relate to previously completed work.", "dim"))
   220→        return
   221→
   222→    # Detect recurring patterns
   223→    recurring = resolved_services.detect_recurring_patterns(
   224→        si.open_issues,
   225→        si.resolved_issues,
   226→    )
   227→    recurring_dims = sorted(recurring.keys())
   228→
   229→    # If recurring patterns exist, report must mention at least one dimension
   230→    if not _validate_recurring_dimension_mentions(
   231→        report=report,
   232→        recurring_dims=recurring_dims,
   233→        recurring=recurring,
   234→    ):
   235→        return
   236→
   237→    # Save stage
   238→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
   239→    reflect_stage = {
   240→        "stage": "reflect",
   241→        "report": report,
   242→        "cited_ids": [],
   243→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
   244→        "issue_count": issue_count,
   245→    }
   246→    reflect_stage["recurring_dims"] = recurring_dims
   247→    stages["reflect"] = reflect_stage
   248→
   249→    # Jump-back: preserve or clear confirmation
   250→    if is_reuse and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("confirmed_at"):
   251→        stages["reflect"]["confirmed_at"] = existing_stage["confirmed_at"]
   252→        stages["reflect"]["confirmed_text"] = existing_stage.get("confirmed_text", "")
   253→    cleared = cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages, "reflect")
   254→
   255→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   256→
   257→    reflect_detail = {
   258→        "issue_count": issue_count,
   259→        "reuse": is_reuse,
   260→    }
   261→    reflect_detail["recurring_dims"] = recurring_dims
   262→    resolved_services.append_log_entry(
   263→        plan,
   264→        "triage_reflect",
   265→        actor="user",
   266→        detail=reflect_detail,
   267→    )
   268→    resolved_services.save_plan(plan)
   269→
   270→    print_reflect_result(
   271→        issue_count=issue_count,
   272→        recurring_dims=recurring_dims,
   273→        recurring=recurring,
   274→        report=report,
   275→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   276→        cleared=cleared,
   277→        stages=stages,
   278→    )
   279→
   280→
   281→def _cmd_stage_organize(
   282→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   283→    *,
   284→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   285→) -> None:
   286→    """Record the ORGANIZE stage: validates cluster enrichment.
   287→
   288→    Instead of gating on a text report, validates that the plan data
   289→    itself has been enriched: each manual cluster needs description +
   290→    action_steps. This forces the agent to actually think about each
   291→    cluster's execution plan.
   292→    """
   293→    report: str | None = getattr(args, "report", None)
   294→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
   295→
   296→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   297→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   298→
   299→    if not has_triage_in_queue(plan):
   300→        print(colorize("  No planning stages in the queue — nothing to organize.", "yellow"))
```

```
   524→def _run_sense_check(

   526→    plan: dict,

   532→    dry_run: bool = False,
   533→    append_run_log=None,
   534→) -> tuple[bool, str]:
   535→    """Run sense-check via two parallel codex subprocess batches.
   536→
   537→    Returns (ok, merged_report) where ok is True on success.
   538→    Launches content batches (one per cluster) and one structure batch
   539→    in parallel.
   540→    """
   541→    from functools import partial
   542→
   543→    from desloppify.app.commands.review._runner_parallel_types import BatchProgressEvent
   544→
   545→    from .codex_runner import _output_file_has_text, run_triage_stage
   546→
   547→    _log = append_run_log or (lambda _msg: None)
   548→
   549→    clusters = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   550→    total_content = len(clusters)
   551→    total = total_content + 1  # +1 for structure
   552→    print(colorize(f"\n  Sense-check: {total_content} content batches + 1 structure batch.", "bold"))
   553→    _log(f"sense-check-parallel content_batches={total_content}")
   554→
   555→    tasks: dict[int, object] = {}
   556→    batch_meta: list[tuple[str, Path]] = []  # (label, output_file)
   557→
   558→    # Content batches — one per cluster
   559→    for i, cluster_name in enumerate(clusters):
   560→        prompt = build_sense_check_content_prompt(

   562→            plan=plan,
   563→            repo_root=repo_root,
   564→        )
   565→        prompt_file = prompts_dir / f"sense_check_content_{i}.md"
   566→        safe_write_text(prompt_file, prompt)
   567→
   568→        output_file = output_dir / f"sense_check_content_{i}.raw.txt"
   569→        log_file = logs_dir / f"sense_check_content_{i}.log"
   570→        batch_meta.append((f"content:{cluster_name}", output_file))
   571→
   572→        if not dry_run:
   573→            tasks[i] = partial(
   574→                run_triage_stage,
   575→                prompt=prompt,
   576→                repo_root=repo_root,
   577→                output_file=output_file,
   578→                log_file=log_file,
   579→                timeout_seconds=timeout_seconds,
   580→                validate_output_fn=_output_file_has_text,
   581→            )
   582→        print(colorize(f"    Content batch {i + 1}: {cluster_name}", "dim"))
   583→        _log(f"sense-check-content batch={i + 1} cluster={cluster_name}")
   584→
   585→    # Structure batch — single global
   586→    structure_idx = total_content
   587→    structure_prompt = build_sense_check_structure_prompt(
   588→        plan=plan,
   589→        repo_root=repo_root,
   590→    )
   591→    prompt_file = prompts_dir / "sense_check_structure.md"
   592→    safe_write_text(prompt_file, structure_prompt)
   593→
   594→    structure_output = output_dir / "sense_check_structure.raw.txt"
   595→    structure_log = logs_dir / "sense_check_structure.log"
   596→    batch_meta.append(("structure", structure_output))
   597→
   598→    if not dry_run:
   599→        tasks[structure_idx] = partial(
   600→            run_triage_stage,
   601→            prompt=structure_prompt,
   602→            repo_root=repo_root,
   603→            output_file=structure_output,
   604→            log_file=structure_log,
   605→            timeout_seconds=timeout_seconds,
   606→            validate_output_fn=_output_file_has_text,
   607→        )
   608→    print(colorize("    Structure batch: global dependency check", "dim"))
   609→    _log("sense-check-structure batch=global")
   610→
   611→    if dry_run:
   612→        print(colorize("  [dry-run] Would execute parallel sense-check batches.", "dim"))
   613→        return True, ""
   614→
   615→    def _progress(event: BatchProgressEvent) -> None:
   616→        idx = event.batch_index
   617→        label = batch_meta[idx][0] if idx < len(batch_meta) else f"batch-{idx}"
   618→        if event.event == "start":
   619→            print(colorize(f"    Sense-check {label} started", "dim"))
   620→            _log(f"sense-check-batch-start {label}")
   621→        elif event.event == "done":
   622→            elapsed = event.details.get("elapsed_seconds", 0) if event.details else 0
   623→            status = "done" if event.code == 0 else f"failed ({event.code})"
   624→            tone = "dim" if event.code == 0 else "yellow"
   625→            print(colorize(f"    Sense-check {label} {status} in {int(elapsed)}s", tone))
   626→            _log(f"sense-check-batch-done {label} code={event.code} elapsed={int(elapsed)}s")
   627→
   628→    def _error_log(batch_index: int, exc: Exception) -> None:
   629→        _log(f"sense-check-batch-error batch={batch_index} error={exc}")
   630→
   631→    failures = execute_batches(
   632→        tasks=tasks,
   633→        options=BatchExecutionOptions(run_parallel=True, heartbeat_seconds=15.0),
   634→        progress_fn=_progress,
   635→        error_log_fn=_error_log,
   636→    )
   637→
   638→    if failures:
   639→        print(colorize(
   640→            f"  Sense-check: {len(failures)} batch(es) failed: {failures}",
   641→            "red",
   642→        ))
   643→        _log(f"sense-check-parallel-failed failures={failures}")
   644→        return False, ""
   645→
   646→    # Merge outputs
   647→    parts: list[str] = []
   648→    for label, output_file in batch_meta:
   649→        content = ""
   650→        if output_file.exists():
   651→            try:
   652→                content = output_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace").strip()
   653→            except OSError:
   654→                content = "(output missing)"
   655→        if not content:
   656→            content = "(no output)"
   657→        parts.append(f"## {label}\n\n{content}")
   658→
   659→    merged = "\n\n---\n\n".join(parts)
   660→    print(colorize(f"  Sense-check: merged {total} batch outputs ({len(merged)} chars).", "green"))
   661→    _log(f"sense-check-parallel-done merged_chars={len(merged)}")
   662→    return True, merged
   663→
   664→
```

### **GAP 1: Shell Steps with Vague Detail (Primary Issue)**
**Location:** `_stage_validation.py` lines 393-413, enforced in `confirmations.py` lines 521-529

**What the prompt asks for:**
- Lines 248-249 in `stage_prompts.py`: "Every step MUST have `--detail` with 80+ chars INCLUDING at least one file path"
- Lines 268-280: Detail should include "file path, the specific location (line range or function name), and the exact change to make"

**What validation actually enforces:**
```python
def _steps_with_vague_detail(plan: dict, repo_root: Path):
    # Detail is vague if < 80 chars AND no file path
    if len(detail) < 80 and not has_path:
        return vague_step
```

**The gap:** A step can pass validation with detail like:
- "Update shell script to handle errors properly" (89 chars, no file path mention but just long enough)
- The check is `len(detail) < 80 and not has_path` — so if either condition is false, it passes
- A 79-char detail with a single file path mention passes validation
- A 100-char detail with zero file paths but scattered text passes validation

**Corner-cutting opportunity:** Subagent can write detail that technically passes the regex check for file paths (mentions `src/` once) but provides zero actionable guidance.

```python
    missing = sum(
        1 for s in steps
        if isinstance(s, dict) and not s.get("issue_refs")
    )
```

**The gap:** 
- Validation only checks presence, not correctness
- A step can reference ANY issue_refs value — even issue IDs that don't exist
- **No verification that referenced issues are actually open** (could reference resolved/skipped issues)

**Corner-cutting opportunity:** Subagent can:
- Reference a stale issue ID not in the current open set
- Reference a resolved issue to make a step appear "done"
- Use fake issue hashes that don't correspond to any real finding

### **GAP 5: File Path Validation is Lenient**
**Location:** `_stage_validation.py` lines 256-279, enforced in `confirmations.py` lines 487-497

**What the prompt asks for:**

**What validation actually enforces:**
```python
def _steps_with_bad_paths(plan: dict, repo_root: Path):
    for path_str in _PATH_RE.findall(detail):  # regex: src|supabase/...
        p = repo_root / path_str
        if p.exists():
            continue
        alt_ext = _EXT_SWAPS.get(p.suffix)
        if alt_ext and p.with_suffix(alt_ext).exists():
            continue
        bad.append(path_str)
```

**The gap:**
- Only validates paths that match the regex `src/` or `supabase/`
- If detail mentions a file path that doesn't match this regex, it's silently ignored
- A step referencing `lib/foo.ts` (no `src/` prefix) won't be caught
- The regex path check is very forgiving (allows both `.ts` and `.tsx`, etc.)
- **No validation that the file path is actually relevant to the detail's claimed action**

**Corner-cutting opportunity:** Subagent can:
- Write detail mentioning nonexistent files using paths outside `src/supabase`

- List files that exist but are completely unrelated to the step

### **GAP 10: Step Detail Vagueness Check Uses AND Instead of OR**
**Location:** `_stage_validation.py` lines 408-412

**Critical logic bug:**
```python
has_path = bool(_PATH_RE.search(detail))
if len(detail) < 80 and not has_path:  # ← This is AND
    results.append((name, i + 1, ...))
```

**The gap:**
- A step with 100 chars of pure prose and NO file path passes (length check fails)
- A step with 50 chars and a single `src/foo.ts` mention passes (path check fails)
- The validator requires BOTH short detail AND no path to flag as vague
- Should be: `if (len(detail) < 80 or not has_path)` or similar

**But wait — this is actually intentional by design:**
- The validation is: "vague if BOTH short AND no path"
- This allows long descriptions without file paths
- This allows short descriptions with file paths

**The real gap:** What if detail is:
- 200 chars, mentions 3 files, but is completely incoherent about what to do?
- Has a file path but describes the wrong file entirely?
- No validation of semantic coherence

```python
cited = stages["observe"].get("cited_ids", [])
issue_count = stages["observe"].get("issue_count", 0)
```

**The gap:**

- For 40 issues: min = 4

## Summary Table: Validation Gap Severity

| Gap | Severity | Blocking? | Exploitable By Subagent |
|-----|----------|-----------|-------------------------|
| 1. Shell steps vague detail | Critical | Blocks enrich | Can write 79-char vague text with minimal file path |
| 2. Missing issue refs (unchecked) | High | Blocks enrich | Can reference non-existent or wrong issues |
| 3. Shallow steps gate is OR not AND | Medium | Blocks enrich | Can skip detail if issue_refs present |
| 4. Effort tags not validated | Medium | Advisory | Can mismatch effort to actual scope |
| 5. File path validation lenient | Medium | Blocks enrich | Can mention wrong files or out-of-scope paths |
| 6. Priority/ordering not checked | High | No gate | Can create unordered/cyclic clusters |
| 7. Skip decisions not validated | High | No gate | Can skip without justification |
| 8. Auto-attestation is fake | **Critical** | Auto-confirms | Orchestrator bypasses all validation |
| 9. No pilot execution check | High | No gate | No validation code is executable |
| 10. Vagueness uses AND logic | Low | Blocks enrich | Counterintuitive but mostly works |
| 11. Citation ratio too low | Medium | Blocks confirm | Only 5% of issues cited in 100-issue observe |
| 12. Dimension mentions text-match | Medium | Blocks reflect | Substring collisions possible |
| 13. Cluster warnings non-blocking | Medium | Advisory | Bad clustering passes silently |
| 14. Issue_refs not tied to cluster | Medium | No gate | Cross-cluster contamination possible |
| 15. Effort mismatch not checked | Low | Advisory | Effort tag correlation not validated |

## Most Impactful Corner-Cutting Paths

### **Path A: Skip Core Work (via Gap 8 + 2)**
1. Subagent produces minimal clusters with vague steps
2. Steps reference fake or resolved issue IDs (Gap 2)
3. Orchestrator auto-confirms with fake attestation (Gap 8)
4. No human review ever happens
5. Plan quality is garbage but confirms successfully

### **Path B: Hide Bad Organization (via Gap 6 + 13)**
1. Create many overlapping clusters (Gap 13 - advisory only)
2. No dependency ordering (Gap 6 - no validation)
3. Steps in wrong order, would fail at runtime (Gap 9)
4. Organize stage confirms because all the blocking gates are about data presence, not quality

### **Path C: Defer Hard Work (via Gap 7 + 11)**
1. Observe stage cites only 5% of issues, superficially (Gap 11)
2. Reflect stage skips 80% of issues with minimal justification (Gap 7)
3. Remaining issues organized but not thoroughly analyzed
4. All gates pass because justification validation is weak

---

## Critical Recommendations

The **most critical gap** is **Gap 8: Auto-Attestation**. This completely bypasses the human confirmation loop for automated runs. The orchestrator (lines 449-460 of `orchestrator.py`) generates a fake attestation and calls `_cmd_confirm_stage` which always succeeds because the text is fabricated to match the validation regex.

The second-most critical is **Gap 2 & 7**: Issue reference and skip decision validation are purely structural, with no semantic validation.

The third is **Gap 6**: No enforcement of cluster priority or execution order, which means a plan can be valid but unexecutable.
